### [1] [Critical collapse remains discretely self-similar at large D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07007)
*Christian Ecker,Florian Ecker,Daniel Grumiller*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈登模型在高维时空中的临界坍缩行为，发现离散自相似性回响周期Δ在无限维极限下仍保持有限，并给出Δ随维度D变化的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验近期关于高维极限下回响周期Δ是否发散的猜想，探索高维引力临界现象的普适性。

Method: 结合解析推导与高精度数值模拟（最高达D=200维），并构建显式的高维近似模型。

Result: 发现Δ在D→∞时趋于有限值；得到拟合公式Δ=1.6439+10.37/D-O(1/D²)；证伪了Δ在无限维发散的猜想。

Conclusion: 离散自相似性在极高维仍存在，表明该临界现象具有强鲁棒性；高维极限为研究引力临界行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: When matter collapses at the threshold of black hole formation spacetime typically has an emergent continuous or discrete symmetry, depending on the matter content. For the Einstein-Klein-Gordon model in any finite dimension D>3 the associated critical spacetime solutions exhibit discrete self-similarity, characterized by the echoing period Delta. Combining analytical and numerical results with dimensions up to D=200 we predict Delta remains finite at infinite dimension D, contrary to a recent conjecture. We find the fit Delta=1.6439+10.37/D-O(1/D^2) and provide an explicit large-D model.

</details>


### [2] [Analytic Marginalization Over Calibration Uncertainties in Gravitational Wave Inference and Application to Black Hole Ringdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07023)
*David Dzingeleski,Aaron Zimmerman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在时域中使用样条基函数对引力波探测器校准不确定性进行解析边缘化的扩展方法，并将其应用于黑洞衰荡信号（如GW250114和GW250207）的分析，结果表明校准边缘化对参数推断（如剩余质量与自旋）具有一致性影响，但对第二衰荡模式的证据支持有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器校准的不确定性是引力波推断中一种系统误差来源，虽通常较小，但仍需建模并边缘化以提高可靠性。

Method: 将标准频域校准边缘化方法扩展至时域，采用样条基函数实现解析边缘化，并应用于黑洞衰荡的时域分析。

Result: 对GW250114仍发现强第二衰荡模式证据；对GW250207，在纳入校准不确定性后，衰荡推断的剩余质量与自旋与全信号推断一致，但未发现第二模式证据，广义相对论偏差约束较弱。

Conclusion: 所提时域校准边缘化方法有效且必要，尤其在校准不确定性显著时（如GW250207的LIGO-Hanford），能保障多探测器联合分析的一致性，但对高信噪比事件中多模态检验的提升仍有限。

Abstract: The uncertain calibration of gravitational wave detectors is one source of systematic error in gravitational wave inference. While generally small, this uncertainty can be accounted for by modeling and marginalizing over calibration corrections. In this work we extend the standard frequency-domain approach to calibration marginalization to the time domain using basis splines, in such a way that the marginalization is analytic. We apply this method to the time-domain analysis of black hole ringdown, in particular the two highest signal-to-noise events detected so far, GW250114 and GW250207. For GW250114 we continue to find strong evidence for two ringdown modes, after marginalizing over the small calibration uncertainty at the time of the event. For GW250207, the calibration of the LIGO-Hanford detector was uncertain, and calibration marginalization is required for a coherent analysis using both LIGO detectors. We find that when calibration effects are included the remnant mass and spins inferred during ringdown agree with inferences from the full signal. Despite the high signal-to-noise of GW250207, we find no evidence for the presence of a second mode in the ringdown, and as a result deviations from relativity during ringdown are not well constrained.

</details>


### [3] [Testing the Kerr hypothesis with orbital frequencies: lessons from spacelike singularities and black bounces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07024)
*Hooman Neshat,Stefano Liberati*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了黑洞内视界不稳定性的两种可能量子修正结果：一种是形成类空间奇点（Gürses--Gürsey几何），另一种是形成正则黑洞反弹（旋转Simpson--Visser几何）；两者均可拟合Kerr黑洞的光环和ISCO频率，但在能量条件和准正规模上有显著差异，表明仅依赖部分观测量不足以确认天体是否为Kerr黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与半经典不稳定性表明Kerr黑洞内视界需被修正，量子反作用可能导致Planck尺度奇点或更温和的正则黑洞反弹，亟需研究其可观测效应以区分真实黑洞结构。

Method: 采用Gürses--Gürsey（含类空间奇点）和旋转Simpson--Visser（正则黑洞反弹）两类几何模型，对比其光环、ISCO频率、能量条件满足区域及标量场准正规模，并与Kerr解进行系统比较。

Result: 两种模型均可匹配Kerr的光环与ISCO频率（前者靠参数校准，后者天然一致）；但均违反零/类时收敛条件（Gürses--Gürsey限于 horizon 外有限区域，Simpson--Visser 全局违反）；其标量准正规模的阻尼率显著偏离Kerr（前者增强，后者减弱）。

Conclusion: 仅在部分观测量（如光环频率）上与Kerr一致，并不能证实致密天体即为Kerr黑洞；需结合能量条件检验、准正规模等多信使观测进行互补验证。

Abstract: The inner horizon of stationary black holes is subject to classical and semiclassical instabilities, suggesting that the Kerr interior should be modified. Quantum backreaction may drive the curvature to the Planck scale, producing a spacelike singularity, or halt its growth at lower curvatures, leading to a regular black bounce. We investigate the phenomenology of these two possibilities using, respectively, a Gürses--Gürsey geometry with a spacelike singularity and the rotating Simpson--Visser black bounce. Both geometries reproduce the light ring and ISCO frequencies of a Kerr black hole with the same ADM mass and angular momentum, by calibration in the first case and identically in the second. We find that the null and timelike convergence conditions are violated outside the horizon, up to a finite radius beyond the light ring in the Gürses--Gürsey geometry and everywhere in the rotating Simpson--Visser spacetime. Moreover, their fundamental scalar quasinormal modes deviate from those of Kerr, most markedly in the damping, which is enhanced in the Gürses--Gürsey geometry and reduced in the rotating Simpson--Visser one. Thus, agreement with Kerr in a limited set of observables is not sufficient to establish the Kerr nature of a compact object, motivating complementary observational tests.

</details>

*Shoshauna Gauvin,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在量子控制的透明球壳时空中的Unruh-DeWitt探测器响应，通过构造共入射散射标识和正定Gram矩阵，分析了联合、条件与退相位响应，并提出相位门协议补偿自由传播效应；结果显示存在显著的条件响应变化（最高达34.15%），但这些是模型依赖的、关系性的探测器响应，而非背景无关的量子几何探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在非平凡时空结构（如球壳时空）中，局域平坦几何下的探测器如何响应全局匹配的场模，以及量子控制时空叠加对探测器响应的影响，以区分真正的量子引力效应与模型依赖的探测器响应。

Method: 构建量子控制的成对透明球壳时空（具有相同ADM质量），采用紧凑开关的Unruh-DeWitt探测器；引入共入射散射标识将分支激发置于同一Hilbert空间；利用正定Gram矩阵计算联合、条件与退相位响应；设计相干相位门协议补偿探测器时钟自由演化；扩展至偏心与等半径情形，分析响应的关系性与对称性。

Result: 获得中心对称情形下精确s波响应；等半径基准显示条件响应变化达25%；半径与位置组合产生反射奇对称响应，在关系中点消失；归一化态对比度达34.15%，时钟分辨的探针影响对比度达8.09%；所有效应均存在于满足主能量条件的核心区域。

Conclusion: 所观察到的响应效应是关系性、模型依赖的探测器物理结果，不构成背景无关的量子几何见证；一阶系数具有点状极限，有限耦合结论依赖于固定共享截断。

Abstract: A detector inside a static spherical shell samples globally matched field modes even though its local geometry is flat. We construct the response of a compactly switched Unruh-DeWitt detector on a quantum-controlled pair of transparent spherical-shell spacetimes with common Arnowitt-Deser-Misner mass. A stipulated common-incoming scattering identification places the branch excitations in one Hilbert space. Their positive Gram matrix determines joint, conditional, and dephased detector responses at a finite common readout, retaining the physical detector-clock phase in the uncompensated protocol. An additional coherent phase-gate protocol compensates free detector-clock propagation while preserving the shell interaction response. For concentric shells the centered response is exactly $s$ wave. A common incoming direction basis extends the construction to a lab-fixed detector and displaced shell centers, preserving common-translation covariance. The equal-radius benchmark exhibits conditional changes of order $25\%$ without dark-port amplification. Combining radius and position produces an assignment-odd response that reverses under reflection and vanishes at the relational midpoint. The relative normalized-state contrast reaches $34.15\%$ among the computed samples; the relative clock-resolved probe-influence contrast reaches $8.09\%$ for the declared readout, while larger values belong to its separate shared-phase envelope. These effects already occur in the dominant-energy-condition-compatible core. They are relational, model-dependent detector responses, not a background-independent witness of quantum geometry. First-order coefficients have finite pointlike limits; finite-coupling statements are made only at a fixed shared regulator.

</details>


### [5] [Unruh-DeWitt detectors in a delocalized massive shell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07058)
*Giovanna F. do Valle,Saadat Salman Shariff,Natália S. Möller,Nelson Yokomizo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个模型，研究探测器对处于位置叠加态的中空球壳内部真空涨落的响应。利用量子参考系框架，将问题转换为源质量位置确定而探测器处于叠加态的情形，从而可在确定弯曲时空上用标准量子场论工具分析探测器动力学；结果发现，在适当后选择下，探测器响应函数呈现干涉图样，并识别出壳层诱导干涉项最大化的参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力与量子力学交叉领域中，质量处于量子叠加态时如何影响周围时空及真空结构，特别是探测器能否读取源质量量子态的信息。

Method: 采用量子参考系形式主义，将原问题变换至源质量位置确定、探测器处于位置叠加的参考系中，在该系中应用弯曲时空量子场论计算探测器响应函数。

Result: 计算了多种壳层与探测器构型下的响应函数；发现经适当后选择，响应函数出现干涉图样；识别出壳层对干涉项贡献最大的参数区域。

Conclusion: 探测器响应可编码源质量的量子叠加信息，尤其在特定参数和后选择条件下，干涉效应显著，表明可通过局域探测器间接探测量子引力效应。

Abstract: We construct a model for the response of a detector to vacuum fluctuations inside a massive hollow shell in a superposition of positions. The delocalized source mass gives rise to a superposition of spacetime geometries and their associated vacuum fluctuations. To describe this system, we employ the formalism of quantum reference frames to transition to a frame in which the source mass occupies a definite position while the detector is in a superposition of positions. This allows the dynamics of the detector to be analyzed using standard tools of quantum field theory on a definite curved spacetime. We compute the detector response for a wide range of configurations of the shell and detector positions and investigate how information about the quantum state of the shell is encoded in the response function, identifying features of the superposition that are effectively captured by the detector. In particular, we show that, under appropriate postselection, the response function exhibits interference patterns, and identify regions of the parameter space of the model in which the shell-induced contributions to the interference terms are maximized.

</details>


### [6] [The Goldilocks Prior: Physically Consistent Inference of Neutron Star Properties from Gravitational Wave Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07146)
*Sanika Khadkikar,B. S. Sathyaprakash*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为'Goldilocks Prior'的新型物理启发先验，用于引力波观测中中子星参数（特别是潮汐形变参数）的准确推断。该先验基于广泛半参数状态方程集合，考虑了啁啾质量、质量比与双潮汐参数之间的联合相关性，并适配探测器灵敏度。模拟结果表明，相比均匀先验和以往的状态方程启发先验，该方法能系统性降低推断偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中子星参数推断方法常使用忽略参数间相关性的先验，或不一致地处理潮汐自由度，导致推断结果存在偏差，影响核物理、天体物理及多信使研究的可靠性。

Method: 构建一个基于半参数状态方程集合、显式建模啁啾质量、质量比及双潮汐参数联合相关性的物理启发先验（Goldilocks Prior），并结合不同质量对称性、自旋状态和信噪比的模拟双中子星并合信号进行验证。

Result: Goldilocks Prior在各类模拟场景下均显著降低了潮汐参数后验分布的推断偏差，优于均匀先验和已有EoS启发先验。

Conclusion: Goldilocks Prior为中子星参数估计提供了更稳健、系统的框架，可提升当前及未来引力波观测中致密物质性质推断的可靠性。

Abstract: Gravitational wave observations of neutron star mergers provide a unique window into the interiors of neutron stars, probing dense matter from microscopic nuclear interactions to the macroscopic structure, including their maximum mass, beyond which the remnant collapses to a black hole. Accurate inference of these properties is crucial, as many astrophysical, nuclear, and multimessenger studies rely on gravitational wave constraints. Studies on estimating neutron-star properties from gravitational waves often employ priors that neglect correlations between parameters, or treat tidal degrees of freedom inconsistently, leading to biases in inferred neutron-star properties. We introduce a novel physically informed prior, Goldilocks Prior, that is informed by a broad, semi-parametric ensemble of equations of state, accounts for the joint correlations among the chirp mass, mass ratio, and both tidal deformability parameters, and is tailored to detector sensitivity. Using several mock binary neutron star merger simulations spanning symmetric and asymmetric masses, spinning and non-spinning systems, and a range of signal-to-noise ratios, we show that Goldilocks Prior consistently reduces biases in the inferred tidal posteriors relative to uniform and previously proposed EoS-informed priors. This framework provides a robust, systematic approach for neutron star parameter estimation, enabling more reliable inference for both current and future gravitational wave observations.

</details>


### [7] [Reanalysis of GW170817 and GW190425 under the Goldilocks Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07147)
*Sanika Khadkikar,B. S. Sathyaprakash*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出并应用了一种名为'Goldilocks Prior'的新先验方法，用于分析双中子星并合引力波事件（GW170817和GW190425）的潮汐形变参数，并评估其对致密物质状态方程（EoS）推断的影响。结果表明，该先验显著改变单事件参数推断（尤其对低信噪比事件GW190425），但联合EoS约束仍由高信噪比事件主导而基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯推断中使用的均匀先验可能引入系统性偏差，尤其在低信噪比下；需发展能反映物理相关性且适配探测器灵敏度的更合理先验。

Method: 提出并应用‘Goldilocks Prior’——融合大量候选状态方程预测的相关性，并按探测器灵敏度展宽；将其应用于GW170817与GW190425的数据分析；再通过谱表示法和高斯过程两种分层方法，将事件似然传播至状态方程空间。

Result: 对GW170817，轻质量中子星潮汐形变参数Λ₂最概然值从约215升至约443；对GW190425，允许的Λ范围显著收窄；单事件EoS约束在两种先验下对GW170817一致，但对GW190425则存在超出数值涨落的差异。

Conclusion: 先验选择显著影响低信噪比事件的似然恢复和单源参数推断，但联合EoS约束因GW170817主导而稳健；Goldilocks Prior提升了物理一致性与推断可靠性。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from binary neutron star mergers encode the properties of the densest matter in the universe, but recovering them accurately requires controlling biases introduced by the inference framework. The recently developed Goldilocks Prior incorporates correlations predicted by a large ensemble of candidate equations of state and broadens them according to the expected detector sensitivity. We apply this framework to GW170817 and GW190425 and find that the inferred component tidal deformabilities differ substantially from standard analyses. For GW170817, we infer both neutron stars to be more tidally deformable than previously reported, with the most probable value for the lighter star nearly doubling from $Λ_2\simeq215$ to $Λ_2\simeq443$ relative to the uniform-prior analysis. For GW190425, the Goldilocks Prior considerably reduces the range of allowed tidal deformabilities. We propagate the resulting event likelihoods to the dense matter equation of state using two complementary hierarchical methods, a strongly parametrized spectral representation and a broader semi-parametric Gaussian-process ensemble. For GW170817, both prior treatments yield consistent EoS constraints despite substantial differences in the inferred properties of the individual neutron stars. For GW190425, however, the EoS constraints differ beyond numerical scatter even after removing the source-level priors, showing that prior choice affects likelihood recovery for low-SNR events. The joint EoS constraints nevertheless remain largely unchanged because GW170817 dominates the inference.

</details>

*A. T. N. Silva,M. A. Anacleto,Ali Övgün,L. Casarini*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索替代黑洞的致密天体模型（如gravastar）的可观测特征，特别是如何通过引力透镜、红移和成像等天文观测手段区分其与传统黑洞模型（如Schwarzschild-de Sitter）的差异，并厘清不同理论形变参数（非对易尺度与KS尺度）的独立可观测性。

Conclusion: gravastar的光学可观测量（尤其是强引力透镜高阶特征）能有效打破不同量子引力启发形变参数间的简并，为未来高分辨率引力波与事件视界望远镜观测提供关键理论判据，支持用天文数据检验替代理论模型。

Abstract: We construct a thin-shell gravastar with a de Sitter interior and a phenomenological exterior combining a Lorentzian noncommutative-inspired mass distribution with the unexpanded Kazakov--Solodukhin deformation. Within an effective Einstein description, we derive the surface stresses, evaluate the shell energy conditions, and compute transparent-shell geodesics using a single exterior metric. The associated horizon-bearing family satisfies a radial-pressure thermodynamic identity with area entropy. For the gravastar, the first three penetrating visibility boundaries move inward with increasing KS scale, while the exterior photon orbit and critical impact parameter move outward. A finite-observer backlighting calculation reproduces the Schwarzschild--de Sitter benchmark and tracks the merger of visibility boundaries as the shell moves beyond the photon orbit. We also compute the redshift-weighted intensity of a face-on, optically thin emitting layer. Additional disk crossings after core transmission produce inner image structure whose brightness depends on the radial emission profile. At leading inverse-radius order, both deformation scales enter through one coefficient, producing an exact geometric parameter degeneracy. We show that the next inverse-radius term breaks this degeneracy: the critical impact parameter and logarithmic strong-deflection coefficient provide independent local information about the two scales. Full-metric calculations for equal-leading-coefficient parameter pairs resolve this splitting and quantify the accuracy of the higher-order expansion.

</details>


### [9] [Scalar perturbations in two-scalar-field warm inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07193)

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the tidal Love numbers (TLNs) of several classes of exotic compact objects (ECOs) using the recently derived full perturbed junction conditions. We also find that the signs of the TLNs can differ qualitatively from previous results, especially in the odd-parity sector. Abstract: Detecting single gravitons is possible through the extraction of discrete energy quanta from passing gravitational waves. For decades, this was considered hopeless, with estimates suggesting a detector the size of Jupiter. Here, I explain how these earlier estimates relied on the highly inefficient electron ionization process, while the absorption through the full mass quadrupole of a macroscopic system can bring graviton detection within experimental reach. Operating in the LIGO frequency band solves the challenges of graviton sources and noise suppression, allowing gravitons to be extracted from known passing gravitational waves and detection events to be heralded by classical gravitational-wave observations. Combined with the ability to resolve single energy quanta in macroscopic systems, such as in quantum acoustics, this opens a realistic path toward graviton detection. I also discuss the general limitations of particle detection in establishing an underlying quantum theory, illustrated by an energy-nonconserving semiclassical alternative. Nevertheless, the semiclassical alternative is not standard classical wave physics, and extracting $E=\hbarω$ from a passing wave constitutes bona fide particle detection. This opens the way not only to the first detection of individual gravitons, but also to experimentally probing linearized quantum gravity and the graviton counting statistics of gravitational waves.

Dark energy perturbations stay several orders of magnitude below matter perturbations, yet the noncanonical sound speed still alters the Hubble friction term, raising $S_8$ by only $\approx0.5σ$ relative to $Λ$CDM. Abstract: The vast majority of cosmological simulations employ periodic boundary conditions to achieve statistical homogeneity and to mimic the infinite spatial extent of our Universe. In a cubic periodic domain the Green function breaks full rotational invariance and introduces a geometry-induced anisotropy in the gravitational interaction. We quantify how this intrinsic anisotropy affects cosmic structure growth and investigate the extent to which it biases the commonly used large-scale structure and halo-based observables in dark-matter-only simulations. We analyse an ensemble of 498 cosmological dark-matter-only $N$-body simulations in cubic $T^3$ topological manifolds, evolved to $z=0$, spanning box sizes from $L=6.25\,\mathrm{Mpc}$ to $L=100\,\mathrm{Mpc}$ at fixed mass and force resolution. We compare anisotropy-sensitive statistics across the suite, including the matter power spectrum, halo abundance, merger histories, shapes and alignments, and filament orientations. Finite periodic boundaries imprint orientation-dependent signatures aligned with the box geometry. These signatures propagate beyond the largest modes and can induce systematic shifts in both field-level and halo-level statistics. The detectable effects propagate to scales much smaller than the box size. Our results demonstrate that periodic-box anisotropy is a non-negligible source of systematics for small simulation volumes, including those commonly employed in modern hydrodynamical simulations.

Abstract: The dispersion measures (DMs) of fast radio bursts (FRBs) trace ionised baryons along the line of sight, probing baryonic feedback. Two complementary observables can be constructed from an FRB catalogue: the DM-redshift (Macquart) relation of host-identified FRBs, and the angular power spectrum of the DM field of the full catalogue. Neglecting the cross-covariance changes the width of the $χ^2$ distribution by only 0.8 per cent, so the two observables can be treated as effectively independent for wide-area surveys. These tasks require uniformly selected samples extending to low black hole and host-galaxy masses, allowing self-consistent measurements of black hole mass functions, Eddington-ratio distributions, and host properties. Grating spectroscopy, deep MIRI imaging, and variability analysis through repeated observations of the same fields would provide complementary tests of black hole formation and growth, their connection to host-galaxy evolution, and the nature of newly discovered populations of LRDs. In this white paper, we argue that these joint tests require a coordinated JWST Multi-Cycle Treasury program; such a program could provide the depth, uniformity, and time baseline needed to place substantially stronger, complementary constraints on early black hole formation, growth, and their connection to galaxy evolution.

Abstract: We present CosmicGrain, a dust physics module built on Gadget-4 that models explicit dust superparticles that experience resolved gas-dust drag and radiation pressure, undergo collisional heating and cooling with gas, and are further shaped by subgrid treatments for grain growth, thermal sputtering, coagulation, shattering, supernova shock destruction, and astration. Abstract: We present GetGaia 2.0, a public Python package that retrieves the Gaia data around a stellar system and selects its member stars automatically. The local systematic errors of the Gaia reference frame are measured on the quasars of each field. It measures the systemic PMs of 55 dwarf galaxies and 93 globular clusters at a significance of at least $3σ$; 29 of the dwarfs and 86 of the clusters are recovered from their position alone. The PMs agree with the published values within 0.1 mas/yr for 47 of the dwarfs and for all the clusters (median differences of 0.032 and 0.009 mas/yr), with uncertainties equal to those of dedicated studies, and none deviates by more than $3σ$. Different methods and techniques have been developed over the last nearly one hundred years to determine the reddening and to map the solar neighbourhood. The members of open clusters are a consistent population of stars formed together with a homogeneous metallicity. Therefore, the reddening can be estimated in a statistical way, avoiding all problems of single stars and spectral-energy-distribution fitting, for example. Other than for most available works, we can also use more massive upper main-sequence stars for the analysis. We used classical colour-colour diagrams (also known as the Q-method) for (B-V), (BP-RP), (U-B), (U-BP), (U-RP), and (U-G) to determine consistent mean reddening values and their errors. The corresponding standard lines for the fitting are independent of the age and metallicity. We determined colour excesses for 5377 Galactic open clusters covering Galactocentric distances from two to twenty kpc, respectively. The derived values were checked with existing dust and extinction maps. In general, we found good agreement. The solar neighbourhood of +-1500 pc shows a very patchy structure of the dust composition. Our results lay the foundations for constructing a new map of the reddening (dust) distribution and estimating clusters' metallicities by isochrone fitting, keeping the distances and reddening values fixed.

Abstract: The Central Molecular Zone (CMZ) combines warm molecular gas, strong turbulence, and a star-formation rate below that expected from its dense-gas reservoir. The JVLA Ammonia C-configuration K-band Survey (JACKS) is designed to connect these environmental conditions to the thermal and kinematic structure of compact, potentially star-forming gas. JACKS follows the footprint of the ALMA Central Molecular Zone Exploration Survey (ACES) with 31 Very Large Array mosaic fields. Its K-band setup covers the metastable NH$_3$ $(1,1)$--$(5,5)$ inversion transitions, five non-metastable NH$_3$ lines, the 22-GHz H$_2$O maser transition, H64$α$ and H66$α$, and broad continuum windows. The nominal resolutions are approximately $1.5''$ ($0.06$ pc at the Galactic Centre) and $0.4$ km s$^{-1}$ for the NH$_3$ lines. We present preliminary imaging products that demonstrate water-maser self-calibration, compact and extended continuum emission, filamentary NH$_3$ structure, and complex compact sources with multiple velocity components. Tests that combine JACKS with the SWAG and HOPS ammonia surveys recover progressively larger angular scales. These early products establish the survey's ability to connect core-scale gas heating and kinematics to the cloud-scale CMZ environment; quantitative temperature, opacity, and core-population measurements will follow from the uniformly reduced data set.

Abstract: Increased computing power and more efficient codes have allowed cosmological simulations of galaxy formation to grow massively in scale: the analysis of their outputs has consequently become a big data computational challenge. We present Octavius, a high-performance, hybrid-parallel simulation analysis toolkit. Octavius ingests a simulation snapshot with associated halo catalogue, locates galaxies with a 6D friends-of-friends algorithm, then produces an HDF5 catalogue of properties for haloes, subhaloes, and galaxies. The analysis is fast and memory efficient: we compare the performance against the widely used predecessor toolkit, Caesar, and show Octavius delivers order-of-magnitude improvements in speed with a factor of >5 reduction in peak memory. We demonstrate near-linear strong scaling of compute and linear peak memory scaling with the number of halo-assigned particles. Compute times scale with the superlinear computational load of larger haloes; we note the pipeline is presently I/O-limited. The code is written in Python and uses a declarative, plugin-based architecture so as to be easily extensible. Octavius can currently analyse SIMBA, SWIFT-KIARA, SWIFT-EAGLE, SWIFT-COLIBRE, and TNG snapshots; AHF, HBT-HERONS, and SUBFIND halo catalogues are supported. Properties computed include masses, galaxy photometry with dust attenuation, and kinematic, radial, morphological, and virial quantities. Output catalogues are easily accessed through a provided user API. The code is open sourced at https://github.com/jp-duminy/octavius

Abstract: We present analysis of an extended emission line region projected 3.77 kpc from the nucleus of the canonical changing-look AGN Mrk~1018, found in MUSE integral-field observations from the Close AGN Reference Survey. The lower limit on the light travel time is $12{,}300 \pm 2{,}300$ yr, implying that the nucleus has therefore been active for at least this long. This is much longer than the duration of either observed changing-look transition, which occurred over several years. This implies that Mrk 1018's multi-year variations are occurring within a longer term active phase.

Method: 基于星团动力学理论，推导星团级两体弛豫时间；分析冷坍缩过程对质量分层的影响；建立质量分量解耦判据；模拟星团并合对系统结构（如半径）的影响。

Abstract: Massive stellar systems have been detected in the high-redshift Universe via the James Webb Space Telescope using gravitational lensing techniques. Such stellar systems may evolve on short dynamical timescales, despite a formally large 2-body relaxation time. If the stellar system consists of a large number of gravitationally bound clusters, a 2-body relaxation time can be defined for the star clusters in analogy to the 2-body relaxation time for stars, implying a faster evolution determined by the number of stars per cluster. At their formation, clusters will go through a process of "cold collapse" as part of their early, non-collisional, evolution. This phase enhances the mass segregation between the more massive and less massive population of stars. We derive a criteria based on the mass of the two components that determines under which conditions the massive component may even decouple from the cluster itself. Mergers of clusters and multiple mergers on average tend to produce systems with larger cluster radii. For the formation of massive black holes via dense stellar systems, the most promising pathway consists in the cold collapse of a dense massive cluster, after few mergers (or possibly none). In such a cluster with a sufficient number of massive stars, the massive population of stars will dynamically decouple in the central region and go through core collapse, leading to the formation of a central massive object. In the case of multiple mergers, massive but more spatially extended stellar systems will form.

Results: We identify 12 high-confidence members based on energy and angular momentum: one intermediate metal-poor star ([Fe/H] $\sim-1.6$), ten VMP stars, and one extremely metal-poor (EMP) star ([Fe/H] $<-3$). Orbital integrations support an association with the Clouds, while test particles reproduce the debris' broad spatial distribution. Motivation: 磁場研究常受视线方向上多层结构重叠干扰，需准确扣除前景偏振以揭示目标天体（NGC 1333）的真实磁场结构。

Method: 利用约840颗恒星的R波段偏振测量、Gaia DR3/StarHorse距离，结合偏振-距离趋势与Stokes参数分解视线成分；在Stokes空间扣除前景后，采用圆统计与角弥散函数（ADF）表征磁场，并与分子谱线及多波段偏振数据对比。

Result: 识别出160–300 pc的前景偏振和300 pc以外的云成分；校正后磁场方向从西北126.3°平滑变化至东南162.2°；ADF给出湍流/有序比分别为0.054和0.095，对应平面磁场强度约59 μG和46 μG；CO数据显示多速度成分；校正后光学偏振与Planck大尺度一致，而近红外/JCMT-POL2显示致密区局部偏离。

Conclusion: NGC 1333具有相干的云尺度磁场，方向平滑变化，致密区存在局部扰动；证实恒星距离+宽视场光学偏振是有效剥离前景、揭示真实磁场结构的关键方法。

Abstract: Magnetic-field studies of star-forming regions are complicated by overlapping structures along the line of sight. We investigate the large-scale magnetic field toward NGC~1333 in Perseus, accounting for foreground polarization through stellar distances and optical polarimetry.
  Our wide-field $R$-band survey covers nearly one square degree and provides polarization measurements for approximately 840 stars, with distances from Gaia DR3 and StarHorse. We used polarization-distance trends and Stokes parameters to identify line-of-sight components and estimate foreground polarization. After subtracting it in Stokes space, we characterized the field using circular statistics and the angular dispersion function (ADF), comparing it with molecular-line and complementary polarimetric observations.
  The data reveal a polarized foreground at 160-300 pc and a cloud component beyond 300 pc. After correction, the plane-of-sky field rotates smoothly from $126.3°\pm0.8°$ in the northwest to $162.2°\pm0.6°$ in the southeast, remaining well ordered in both regions. The ADF gives turbulent-to-ordered field ratios of $0.054^{+0.025}_{-0.022}$ and $0.095^{+0.021}_{-0.047}$, corresponding to plane-of-sky strengths of $59^{+44}_{-23}$ and $46^{+50}_{-13}~μ\mathrm{G}$. Residual foreground fluctuations may affect these estimates. CO data show multiple velocity components with spatially varying contributions. Corrected optical polarization agrees with the large-scale orientation traced by Planck, while near-infrared and JCMT/POL-2 data reveal localized deviations in denser structures.
  Our results indicate a coherent cloud-scale magnetic field with a smoothly changing orientation, alongside localized variations in dense structures. They demonstrate the value of combining stellar distances and wide-field optical polarimetry to disentangle foreground contributions.

</details>

*Rebecca A. Booth,Jo-Anne Brown,Tom Landecker,Roland Kothes,Tyler Foster,Anna Ordog,Alex Schwaner Hill*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超级气泡的物理结构、动力学演化及其能量来源，特别是CTB 102与周围HII区及恒星形成活动的关系。

Method: 综合使用DRAGONS、Effelsberg、GAMIS和HI4PI等射电巡天数据（总强度、旋转量、HI谱线），并补充WHAM和Ziegenbalg的H-alpha光学数据，以完整描绘超级气泡形态；利用Kompaneets模型拟合其形状与膨胀速度，推断年龄与演化阶段。

Result: CTB 102超级气泡尺寸至少为~170 pc × ~420 pc，上下区域膨胀速率分别为~12 km/s和~22.5 km/s，其拉长结构与速度梯度符合Kompaneets模型，估算年龄为1–6 Myr。

Conclusion: CTB 102超级气泡很可能由当前电离CTB 102的星团驱动，但也可能由更早一代恒星产生，而当前星团是在其壳层中形成的。

Abstract: Superbubbles are vast hot cavities carved out by the stellar winds and supernovae of clusters of high-mass stars. We investigate G92.4+5.0 (the CTB 102 superbubble), a Perseus Arm superbubble associated with the HII region CTB 102. We use new and archival radio observations (total intensity, rotation measure, and HI spectral line) from the Dominion Radio Astrophysical Observatory Synthesis Telescope, combined with single-antenna data from the Effelsberg Medium Latitude Survey, the Global Magneto-Ionic Medium Survey, and the HI4PI survey to provide data on large structures. The upper region of the superbubble is faint in HI. In order to detect its full extent, we supplement our observations with H-alpha data from the Wisconsin H-Alpha Mapper Survey and the Ziegenbalg Survey, and observe H-alpha emission in the upper region. We measure the CTB 102 superbubble to be at least ~170 pc wide and ~420 pc tall, with an expansion rate of ~12 km/s in the lower regions of the superbubble and ~22.5 km/s in the upper regions. The elongated structure and increasing expansion rate with height are consistent with the evolutionary properties of a superbubble described by the Kompaneets model. We fit the Kompaneets model to the CTB 102 superbubble and obtain an estimated age of 1-6 Myr. This supports a possible scenario in which the stellar cluster currently ionizing CTB 102 is the expansion energy source for the superbubble. Alternatively, if the superbubble expanded under the power of a previous generation of stars, the current CTB 102 stellar cluster may have formed in the walls of the superbubble.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的碰撞速率系数对解释星际云中分子的转动发射谱至关重要。

Result: 获得TMC-1中HC5NH+激发的最佳拟合参数：H₂数密度n(H₂)=1.63×10⁵ cm⁻³，H₂柱密度N(H₂)=5.09×10¹¹ cm⁻²，最小化约化χ²=0.87；模型谱线强度与观测高度一致。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解HC5NH+在TMC-1典型物理条件下的碰撞激发与辐射特性提供了关键数据和定量依据。

Abstract: Reliable collisional rate coefficients are crucial for interpreting the rotational emission spectra of molecules in interstellar clouds. In this work, we construct a two-dimensional potential energy surface (PES) for the $\mathrm{HC}_5\mathrm{NH}^+$--He system at the CCSD(T)-F12B/cc-pVTZ-F12 level of theory. Rotationally inelastic cross sections are calculated using the quantum close-coupling (CC) method, and the corresponding collisional rate coefficients are obtained by thermal averaging over a Maxwell--Boltzmann distribution. We use these collisional data in a non-local thermodynamic equilibrium (non-LTE) radiative transfer model to investigate the excitation of $\mathrm{HC}_5\mathrm{NH}^+$ in the cold dark cloud TMC-1. A global $χ^2$ minimization analysis of seven observed lines yields best-fitting values of $n(\mathrm{H}_2)=1.63\times10^{5}$\,cm$^{-3}$ and $N(\mathrm{H}_2)=5.09\times10^{11}$\,cm$^{-2}$, with reduced $χ^2_{\min}=0.87$. The modelled line intensities are in good agreement with the observations. These results provide insight into the collisional excitation and radiative properties of $\mathrm{HC}_5\mathrm{NH}^+$ under the physical conditions prevailing in TMC-1.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用SDSS本地体积测绘仪（LVM）数据，将SMC和LMC的数据分别退化至多个空间分辨率（19–272 pc），通过1D/2D Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验比较强发射线比值分布的变化。

Result: 离子化与密度敏感比值（如[OIII]/Hβ、[SII]/Hα、[NII]/Hα）受分辨率损失影响最显著；SMC比LMC更敏感，42 pc尺度即出现明显偏差；分辨率降低导致诊断图趋于均质化，弥散电离气体尤其敏感。

Conclusion: 亚50 pc分辨率对准确解析低金属丰度星系ISM结构至关重要；在无法获得高分辨率的河外研究中，须谨慎解读发射线比值诊断结果。

Abstract: We investigate how spatial resolution affects emission-line diagnostics in the interstellar medium (ISM) of the Small and Large Magellanic Clouds (SMC and LMC) using SDSS Local Volume Mapper (LVM) data obtained at and degraded to 19, 42, 170 pc (for the SMC) and 17, 68, 272 pc (for the LMC), respectively. We compare the distributions of strong emission-line ratios through 1D and 2D Kolmogorov Smirnov tests. We deduced that diagnostics involving ionization and density sensitive ratios such as [OIII]λ5007/H\b{eta}, [SII]λλ6717+6731/Hα and [NII]λ6584/Hα are the most strongly impacted by resolution loss in this order. The SMC shows significant deviations even from intermediate scales (42 pc), whereas for the LMC, the coarsest resolution (272 pc) remains relatively stable, indicating that the SMC is intrinsically more affected by spatial mixing. Resolution degradation produces a marked homogenization effect: extreme values present at the finest scales are progressively diluted, causing diagnostic diagrams to converge toward their mean loci. Diffuse ionized gas is particularly sensitive to this mixing, while Hα selected ionized regions exhibit more robust behavior across resolutions. The sensitivity of diagnostic diagrams involving emission-line ratios, equivalent widths, and kinematic properties varies with both spatial resolution and galaxy, with the SMC generally showing stronger changes than the LMC. Our results demonstrate that sub-50 pc resolution is required to resolve the detailed structure of low-metallicity galaxies, and they highlight at least the need for careful interpretation of line-ratio diagnostics in extragalactic studies where coarse spatial resolution is unavoidable.

### [65] [Unveiling the Origins of Local Group Galaxies with ELT-SHARP: An Overview of Science Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06963)
*Hai-Feng Wang,Guan-Yu Wang,Peng Yang,Guo-Jun Cheng,Francois Hammer,Martin Lopez-Corredoira,Pei-Bin Chen,Qi-Da Li,Xiao-Peng Liu,Yu-Zheng Wang,Xiao-Yue Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出利用新型近红外光谱仪SHARP（用于极大望远镜及未来空间设施）开展银河系及邻近星系的高分辨率、宽视场化学动力学研究，以检验ΛCDM模型在星系尺度上的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ΛCDM模型在大尺度上成功，但在星系及亚星系尺度存在潜在张力；银河系与本星系群是检验这些差异的理想实验室，需借助穿透尘埃、高分辨、宽视场的近红外光谱能力。

Method: 提出基于SHARP光谱仪的科学方案：包含NEXUS（AO辅助多目标光谱，R~17,000，1.2'×1.2'视场）和VESPER（多积分场单元，亚角秒空间分辨）两种模式，覆盖0.95–2.45 μm，结合Gaia、APOGEE等巡天数据，构建恒星六维相空间图。

Result: 将实现银河系盘、核球、晕及邻近星系（如LMC/SMC）中恒星流、棒、旋臂、翘曲、晕晕相互作用等结构的高精度动力学与化学测绘，约束盘演化、并合历史与暗物质子结构。

Conclusion: SHARP将为ΛCDM模型在最小可观测尺度上提供关键检验，推动星系形成与演化理论的发展。

Abstract: The $Λ$CDM paradigm successfully describes large-scale cosmic structure, yet tensions possibly persist on galactic and sub-galactic scales. The Milky Way and the Local Universe offer a uniquely accessible laboratory to probe these discrepancies through chemo-dynamical studies of resolved stellar populations. Understanding disk assembly, dynamical heating, and galactoseismology requires wide-field coverage and high-angular-resolution near-infrared spectroscopy capable of penetrating dust-obscured regions and resolving crowded stellar environments. We propose science cases exploiting \textsc{SHARP}, a multi-mode near-infrared spectrograph for Extremely Large Telescopes and future space facilities. Operating over $0.95$--$2.45\,μ$m in a single exposure, \textsc{SHARP} enables efficient access to key stellar and gas tracers across the Galactic bulge, disk, halo, and nearby galaxies. The \textsc{NEXUS} mode provides adaptive-optics-assisted multi-object spectroscopy over a $1.2'\times1.2'$ field with spectral resolutions up to $R\sim17{,}000$, enabling gas and stellar kinematics. Complementarily, the \textsc{VESPER} multi-IFU mode delivers spatially resolved spectroscopy in crowded regions, allowing reconstruction of internal kinematics and stellar population gradients on sub-arcsecond scales. Combined with surveys such as \textit{Gaia}, APOGEE, LAMOST, LSST, 4MOST, and ELT--MOSAIC, this project will map six-dimensional phase-space structures of disk and bulge stars, stellar streams, and nearby disk galaxies (e.g. LMC, SMC). The data will enable investigations of bars, spiral arms, warps, flares, and satellite interactions, providing critical constraints on disk evolution, merger history, and dark matter substructure, and providing stringent tests of $Λ$CDM on the smallest accessible scales.

</details>


### [66] [SHARP(ening the) view of unresolved globular clusters in ultra diffuse galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06966)
*Marco Mirabile,Michele Cantiello,Benedetta Di Francesco,Ivan Di Antonio,Gianluca Di Rico,Chiara Buttitta,Marco Gullieuszik,Johanna Hartke,Enrichetta Iodice,Maurizio Paolillo*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UDGs挑战ΛCDM星系形成模型，尤其在暗物质含量和形成历史方面；而现有光谱设备难以在本地星系团以外深度探测其球状星团（GCs），亟需新一代高灵敏、高分辨、高复用能力的观测设备。

Method: 基于对NEXUS@SHARP仪器性能（高空间分辨率+高效多目标光谱复用）的模拟与可行性分析，评估其在ELT上对UDG中GCs进行径向速度和恒星种群测量的能力与观测效率。

Conclusion: NEXUS@SHARP将成为研究UDGs暗物质晕和形成演化的关键利器，有望解决当前ΛCDM框架下UDG相关的核心理论挑战。

Abstract: Ultra-Diffuse Galaxies (UDGs) challenge current galaxy formation models within the ΛCDM framework, particularly regarding their dark matter content and formation histories. Globular Clusters (GCs) serve as excellent tracers for probing the halos of these faint systems; however, current spectroscopic facilities struggle to reach the depth required to fully characterize GC populations beyond the local neighborhood. In this work, we present the scientific potential of NEXUS@SHARP, a near-infrared multi-slit spectrograph proposed on the upcoming Extremely Large Telescope (ELT). We demonstrate that NEXUS is uniquely positioned to overcome current observational limitations by combining high spatial resolution with efficient multiplexing. We show that NEXUS can efficiently help to characterize the GC population in UDGs across diverse environments out to 60 to 70 Mpc - with a few hours of ELT time. These capabilities will allow for precise measurements of radial velocities and stellar populations, enabling robust dynamical mass estimates and distinguishing between competing UDG formation scenario.

TL;DR: 本文提出“创世计划”（Genesis Initiative），呼吁STScI设立多周期巡天（MCT）通道，支持约1000–2000小时的协调性、社区主导型JWST大型巡天，以构建统一、统计可控的早期星系演化遗产数据集，弥补当前分散小项目之不足，并最大化与ALMA、Euclid、Roman等设施的协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST前五年取得突破性发现，但也引发更多关于第一代恒星与星系形成及演化的未解问题；现有小型、异质项目无法提供定量物理理解所需的均匀、统计可控的遗产数据集。

Method: 借鉴哈勃望远镜大型协调巡天的成功先例，提出由天文界共同推动、STScI设立MCT机制，支持长达千小时量级、具有统一且可表征选择函数的多周期联合观测项目。

Result: 论证了开展~1000–2000小时社区主导型JWST大型巡天的科学必要性、时间紧迫性与设施协同优势，并明确指出此类项目在单周期内结构上不可实现。

Conclusion: 应立即启动JWST多周期巡天（MCT）通道，以实现对星系从‘第一缕光’到当今形态演化过程的系统性、定量化理解，奠定未来数十年 extragalactic astrophysics 的基础。

Abstract: The Genesis Initiative is an extragalactic community-wide effort to coordinate white papers in response to the STScI Multi-Cycle Treasury (MCT) concept call. Two of JWST's primary science goals are to discover and characterize the first stars and galaxies, and to understand how they evolved into the galaxies we see today - questions whose fundamental importance has been affirmed by the past three decadal surveys. JWST's first five years have surpassed expectations, revealing a bounty of unexpected discoveries about the early universe. Yet these discoveries have raised as many questions as they have answered, and key mission goals remain unmet. Transforming this excitement into a quantitative physical understanding of early galaxy formation requires a uniform, statistically controlled legacy dataset that the current portfolio of smaller, heterogeneous programs cannot provide. The history of the Hubble Space Telescope offers a clear precedent. Its most transformative extragalactic results came from large, coordinated surveys far exceeding what the standard time-allocation process typically approves, and these uniform datasets remain scientifically productive decades later. We argue that JWST is now poised for the same leap. Answering the field's central open questions demands coordinated, community-led surveys of ~1000-2000 hr, with uniform and well-characterized selection functions that are structurally impossible within single-cycle programs. Such an investment is time-critical while JWST's instruments remain at peak performance, and would maximize synergy with ALMA, Euclid, Roman, Rubin, and future facilities. We therefore urge STScI to open a MCT channel, enabling the bold, ambitious surveys needed to understand how galaxies evolve from first light to the present day.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出利用JWST的NIRSpec进行极深中分辨率光谱巡天，以获取高信噪比（SNR）的高红移（z>1.5）星系连续谱数据，从而约束恒星年龄、金属丰度、动力学及中性气体等关键物理参数，并为星系熄火、质量测量、并合历史及再电离等研究提供支持；同时给出了基于经验的SNR-星等-曝光时间关系及一个1000小时的巡天方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JWST NIRSpec归档数据多聚焦于发射线探测，深度不足以支撑连续谱科学研究；而连续谱高信噪比数据对理解高红移星系的多种物理过程至关重要，且JWST是当前唯一可行平台。

Method: 基于实测数据推导NIRSpec中分辨率下近红外连续谱信噪比（SNR）与星等、曝光时间的经验关系，并验证其红移无关性；据此设计一个1000小时的巡天方案，目标为在z>1.5样本中实现SNR=20/像素；同时讨论关键的天空扣除策略。

Result: 建立了适用于JWST NIRSpec中分辨率观测的连续谱SNR预测模型（与红移无关），提出了可操作的深场连续谱巡天方案，并明确了天空扣除对提升连续谱信噪比的关键作用。

Conclusion: 应紧急优先安排JWST大量观测时间开展极深NIRSpec连续谱巡天，该数据将极大拓展高红移星系多维度物理参数的测量能力，且不可被未来设备替代。

Abstract: We argue for a significant investment of JWST time into an extremely deep medium-resolution NIRSpec MSA survey targeting a large, representative sample of high-redshift $(z>1.5)$ galaxies, specifically intended to obtain high-SNR continuum detections. This is only possible with JWST, and no planned future instrumentation has similar capabilities, meaning that obtaining such observations with JWST must be urgently prioritised. The vast majority of existing archival NIRSpec observations are not deep enough for continuum science, being focused on the detection of emission lines. Deeper continuum data opens up the ability to constrain a much wider range of key galaxy physical parameters, including stellar population ages and metallicities, stellar kinematics and neutral gas physical properties, inflows and outflows. Such measurements will provide crucial insight into quenching physics, galaxy dynamical masses, merger histories, pristine early star-formation, reionization and the physics of Little Red Dots, as well as enabling all of the emission-line science that can be achieved with shallower observations. To facilitate future survey planning, we present an empirically derived relationship between rest-optical continuum SNR in JWST NIRSpec medium-resolution data, rest-optical apparent magnitude and exposure time, which we show to be independent of redshift (and hence the grating used). We then provide a 1000-hour sketch survey plan based on achieving the continuum SNR $=20$/pixel necessary to constrain key physical properties and scaling relations for a large galaxy sample at $z>1.5$. We finally discuss NIRSpec sky subtraction strategies, which are of critical importance for continuum SNR.

</details>


### [69] [Detection of Differential Chromatic Refraction with LSST Camera data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07161)
*Matthew J. Temple,Phoebe N. Lister*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文利用LSST巡天数据，首次在星系和类星体观测中检测到差分色散折射（DCR）效应，并证明DCR可补充多波段颜色信息，提升星系样本选择与光谱红移估计的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球大气像棱镜一样使蓝光比红光更偏向天顶，导致不同波长光线的星位置随天顶距（airmass）变化；LSST高重复、宽airmass覆盖的观测为利用该效应提取额外光谱信息提供了独特机会。

Method: 基于LSST相机试运行数据（Data Preview 2）中COSMOS天区的星系与类星体观测，分析其天顶距依赖的天体位置偏移，识别并量化差分色散折射（DCR）信号。

Result: 成功在真实观测中检测到DCR效应；证明DCR信息可与LSST六波段测光颜色协同，缓解星系选源与光度红移估计中的参数简并性。

Conclusion: DCR是LSST一项尚未被充分利用的宝贵信息源，在未来数据处理中应避免无意剔除该信号，而应将其纳入科学分析流程。

Abstract: The Earth's atmosphere acts like a prism, deflecting blue light towards the zenith more than red light. Airmass-dependent changes in astrometry can therefore be used to infer additional information about the wavelength distribution of light within a photometric passband. The Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST) will observe each point in the southern sky several hundred times, with observations spread across a range of airmasses. Using commissioning data from the LSST camera released in LSST Data Preview 2, we report the detection of this differential chromatic refraction (DCR) effect in observations of galaxies and quasars in the COSMOS field. We show how information from DCR can complement the six-band colour information from LSST and help break degeneracies when selecting extragalactic samples and estimating their photometric redshifts. DCR will be a valuable resource for scientific studies and care should be taken not to remove useful DCR information from future LSST data releases.

</details>


### [70] [MapHub: Daily Relevance Heatmaps of New arXiv Preprints for a Research Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07174)
*Kaili Cao,Camille Avestruz,Feige Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: MapHub 是一个基于大语言模型的 arXiv 预印本推荐系统，每日自动评估新论文对研究社区成员的相关性，并以热力图形式展示，显著优于传统文本匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决研究人员在海量 arXiv 论文中高效发现高相关性工作的难题，提升学术信息筛选效率。

Method: 使用大语言模型（Claude）每日分析 arXiv 新投稿，将其与每位参与者的个性化文献组合（portfolio）进行语义匹配，生成 0–100 的相关性评分并可视化为热力图。

Result: 作者盲评显示，MapHub 评分前 10% 的论文覆盖了评审者自选 top 5% 论文的约 70%，远高于文本匹配基线的约 33%；astro-ph 领域日均约 6% 论文达 30 分，每三天约有一篇达 60 分。

Conclusion: MapHub 是一种轻量、通用、可部署于标准 LLM 订阅的服务，已在天体物理领域验证有效性，适用于任意学科。

Abstract: MapHub, the MIDAS arXiv Preprint Hub, assesses each day's new arXiv submissions for every participant in a research community, with a large language model reading each paper against portfolios built for the participants. It publishes the relevance scores, from 0 to 100, as a heatmap that shows whose work each paper touches. In the authors' blind ratings, the top 10\% of papers by MapHub's scores held about 70\% of the raters' own top 5\%, pooled, against about a third for a text-matching baseline. About 6\% of a day's astro-ph papers reach 30 for each participant, and one every three days reaches 60. MapHub runs on a standard Claude subscription and is designed to be useful for any field: https://github.com/kailicao/maphub-public.

</details>

*Poorva Singh,Mugundhan V.,Anshu Kumari*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出将测角偏振法（Goniopolarimetry）应用于SWARA极化仪，以实现空间天气事件的早期预警；通过三单极子天线仿真，分析其在不同信噪比和视场下的方向到达角（DoA）估计性能，并评估算法对银河背景噪声的敏感性；后续工作将优化单极子有效长度及等离子体环境下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提供空间天气或太阳事件的早期预警，需发展基于电磁波偏振特性的方向到达角（DoA）测量技术——测角偏振法，并将其集成到SWARA极化仪中。

Method: 采用三单极子天线构型进行初步电磁仿真，分析其在不同信噪比（SNR）和视场（FOV）下的响应特性；通过仿真计算角定位误差（ALE）随SNR的变化，量化测角偏振算法对银河背景噪声的灵敏度。

Result: 仿真结果揭示了三单极子天线在不同SNR下的角定位误差变化趋势，为评估该测角偏振算法在真实空间背景噪声下的可行性提供了依据。

Conclusion: 测角偏振法具备应用于SWARA极化仪进行空间天气监测的潜力；后续需优化单极子有效长度以兼顾灵敏度、定位精度与阻抗匹配，并在等离子体环境中进一步验证其鲁棒性。

Abstract: Goniopolarimetry is a technique that measures the direction-of-arrival (DoA) using the polarization properties of the incident electromagnetic (EM) waves. This study aims to incorporate the technique of Goniopolarimetry for our proposed polarimeter, Space Weather Radio (SWARA) Polarimeter, to provide early warnings for space weather or solar events. We have performed preliminary simulations for a tri-monopole antenna configuration. The behaviour of the tri-monopole antenna with varying signal-to-noise (SNR) ratios along the field-of-view (FOV) was investigated using simulations. The angular localization error (ALE) variations, along with SNRs, were simulated to quantify the sensitivity of the Goniopolarimetric algorithm to galactic background noise. Future work will focus on determining the optimum effective length of the individual monopoles to maximize sensitivity and localization accuracy while ensuring efficient impedance matching with the receiver. The optimized antenna will be evaluated under realistic plasma environments through electromagnetic and plasma simulations to investigate the influence of plasma parameters.

</details>


### [72] [GALI Localization of Off-Grid GRB Directions: A Comparison of Rectangular and Triangular Calibration Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07763)
*Julia Saleh-Natur,Ehud Behar,Omer Reich,Shlomit Tarem,Zvika Tarem,Alex Vdovin,Amir Feigenboim,Roey Evental,Solomon Margolin*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三维闪烁体阵列内部阴影调制的伽马暴（GRB）定位仪器GALI，并通过采用基于二十面体球面剖分的三角形校准网格，显著提升了方向重建精度，实验室和蒙特卡洛模拟均验证了其优于传统矩形网格的性能，且具备在CubeSat平台实现度量级定位的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速、精确地定位伽马暴对多波段和多信使后续观测至关重要，但兼顾大视场覆盖与高精度定位仍具挑战性。

Method: GALI利用三维闪烁体阵列内部阴影导致的计数率调制来确定源方向；本文设计并评估了一种基于二十面体球面剖分的三角形校准网格，对比了其与传统矩形网格在校准点各向同性采样和插值性能上的差异，并通过全尺寸探测器实验室测量及50,000次蒙特卡洛模拟（低轨背景、1000计数/暴）验证定位精度。

Result: 实验室中三角形网格平均角距离为0.86°±0.14°，优于矩形网格的1.19°±0.28°；蒙特卡洛模拟中二者分别为1.91°±1.20°和2.73°±2.18°；探测能区为10 keV–~1 MeV，峰值有效面积约130 cm²（100–150 keV）；1U立方星版本仿真亦实现度量级定位。

Conclusion: 三角形校准网格提升了GALI的方向重建精度与鲁棒性，结合其紧凑设计与宽能区响应，验证了其在低成本CubeSat平台上开展伽马暴及多信使科学任务的可行性。

Abstract: Rapid localization of gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) is essential for multi-wavelength and multi-messenger follow-up, but combining wide sky coverage with accurate localization remains challenging. GALI (GAmma-ray burst Localizing Instrument) determines source directions from count-rate modulations caused by internal shadowing in a three-dimensional scintillator array. We investigate a triangular calibration mesh based on an icosphere tessellation of the hemisphere and compare it with the rectangular mesh used previously. The triangular mesh samples the hemisphere more isotropically and gives better interpolation between calibration points. Laboratory measurements with the full-scale GALI detector yield an average angular separation of $0.86^\circ \pm 0.14^\circ$ for random source directions, compared with $1.19^\circ \pm 0.28^\circ$ for the rectangular mesh. Monte Carlo simulations of 50,000 randomly distributed GRBs in realistic low-Earth-orbit background conditions, each with 1000 detected counts, give average angular separations of $1.91^\circ \pm 1.20^\circ$ and $2.73^\circ \pm 2.18^\circ$ for triangular and rectangular meshes, respectively. The better laboratory performance is partly attributed to its approximately 3-4 times higher detected counts. Additional simulations indicate sensitivity from 10 keV to $\sim$1 MeV, with a peak effective area of about 130 cm$^2$ near 100-150 keV. A compact 1U GALI implementation also achieves degree-scale localization in simulations, demonstrating the concept's potential for CubeSat-based GRB and multi-messenger missions.

</details>


### [73] [Design and Commissioning of the BURSTT-256 Main Antenna Array for the Fast Radio Burst Survey in the Local Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07832)
*Shih-Hao Wang,Kai-Yang Lin,Chao-Te Li,Chau-Ching Chiong,Jen-Chieh Cheng,Chung-Cheng Chen,Hsien-Chun Tseng,Shu-Hao Chang,Pai-An Chen,Ta-Shun Wei,Chin-Ting Ho,Vigo Feng-Shun Wu,Yu-Chieh Huang,Sujin Eie,Yao-Huan Tseng,Ue-Li Pen,Chih-Yi Wen,Homin Jiang,Andrew Wang,Bishnu Kumar Sharma,Tzu-Hong Chen Lin,Yuan-Wei Hu,Shih-Wen Chung,Chen-Hui Peng,Ming-Tang Chen,Pisin Chen,Jiwoo Nam,Tomotsugu Goto,Tetsuya Hashimoto,Chin-Ping Hu,Yuan-Chang Hou,Daniel Baker,Michael Chiang,Somnath Dutta,Sun-Kun King,Hiroto Masaoka,Chung-Yun Kuo,Che-Wei Pai,Decmend Fang-Jie Ling*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: BURSTT-256是一个用于探测和精确定位近邻宇宙中快速射电暴（FRBs）的射电干涉阵列，具备约6000平方度的瞬时视场和亚角秒级分辨率，本文介绍了其设计、部署与初步运行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高对近邻宇宙中快速射电暴（FRBs）的探测率和定位精度，以支持多波段与多信使对应体识别，从而揭示FRBs的物理起源。

Method: 构建由256个对数周期偶极子天线（LPDA）组成的主阵列（BURSTT-256），工作频段400–800 MHz，采用实时因子化数字波束合成技术生成256个波束；辅以多个远距小站实现甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）定位；开展射频环境评估、天线系统搭建、前端接收与后端处理系统集成，并利用亮射电源进行在轨校准。

Result: 主阵列已成功部署并完成初步调试，实测系统等效通量密度约为11 kJy（商用天线），优化后（定制天线，预计2026年底）可达约4 kJy；当前视场约3200 deg²，波束合成保真度与灵敏度已通过天文定标源验证。

Conclusion: BURSTT-256是未来扩展至2048单元乃至更大规模阵列的关键探路者，为实现大视场、高精度FRB定位及后续多信使研究奠定了技术与运行基础。

Abstract: The Bustling Universe Radio Survey Telescope in Taiwan (BURSTT) is a radio interferometer array designed for surveying fast radio bursts (FRBs) in the nearby Universe with a wide instantaneous field of view (FoV) of $\approx$6000 deg$^2$ and precisely localizing them with sub-arcsecond angular resolution. The system's on-sky performance, sensitivity, and beamforming fidelity have been calibrated through observations of bright celestial calibrators. The antenna array in the initial phase using commercial LPDAs has a system equivalent flux density of $\approx$11 kJy, and is expected to achieve $\approx$4 kJy using custom-design LPDAs after optimization in late 2026. BURSTT-256 serves as a critical pathfinder for future expansion of the array to 2048 elements and beyond, operating at 300-700 MHz with $\approx$6000 deg$^2$ FoV. It establishes the framework required to discover and localize nearby FRBs with sub-arcsecond precision. By substantially increasing the number of well-localized nearby FRBs, BURSTT will facilitate multi-wavelength and multi-messenger counterpart identification, helping to unveil the elusive origin of FRBs.

Conclusion: K41假设在典型天文台址的大气边界层中极为罕见，尤其在稳定层结下；必须摒弃单一标度律范式，发展考虑热力分层状态的湍流建模与光学参数反演方法，以保障极高分辨率天文观测的精度与可靠性。

Abstract: The atmospheric boundary layer (ABL) presents turbulence regimes that diverge from the classical Kolmogorov 1941 (K41) power-law spectrum widely assumed in optical atmospheric profiling, yet turbulence characterization for astronomical applications remains predominantly based on K41 assumptions. Using high-frequency sonic anemometer data from site-testing campaigns at the Giant Magellan Telescope (GMT) and Thirty Meter Telescope (TMT) sites in Chile, we apply detrended fluctuation analysis (DFA)---a technique from fractal time-series analysis---to identify the inertial sub-range and evaluate statistical isotropy in the ABL, comparing our approach with the Fourier-based algorithm for robust identification of the inertial subrange (ARIIS). Strict Kolmogorov conditions---simultaneous isotropy and $k^{-5/3}$ scaling across all fields---are satisfied in fewer than 7 per cent of observations. Temperature fields systematically decouple from velocity components under stable stratification, exhibiting Bolgiano-Obukhov scaling ($k^{-7/5}$) during nighttime hours, indicating active rather than passive scalar behaviour. DFA-detected regime transitions correlate with characteristic turbulence scales, providing objective, model-free boundaries for the inertial subrange. Comparative $C_n^2$ estimation reveals that structure function methods assuming K41 scaling diverge significantly from flux-based parameterizations under stable conditions. The rarity of true Kolmogorov conditions at these sites necessitates stratification-aware methodologies for accurate optical turbulence characterization at next-generation extremely large telescope facilities.

Abstract: Direct imaging of Earth-like exoplanets requires strong suppression of stellar light at small angular separations, achievable with extreme adaptive optics (XAO). The pyramid wavefront sensor (PWFS) is widely used for its sensitivity but suffers from nonlinearities and limited dynamic range. Modulation extends the range but reduces sensitivity and limits loop speed. We investigate an unmodulated infrared PWFS (IR-umPWFS), where the dynamic range scales with wavelength, removing the need for modulation and enabling faster operation. We adopt a 3-sided PWFS, which eliminates the roof defect of the classical 4-sided design. We assess its feasibility and performance through end-to-end simulations, lab tests, and on-sky observations on the PAPYRUS bench at the Observatoire de Haute-Provence (OHP), and compare it with a visible modulated PWFS (VIS-mPWFS). Simulations show reliable operation, reaching Strehl ratios up to ~60% at 1400 nm for seeing up to 4''. Infrared operation improves linearity, reduces optical-gain variations and modal cross-talk. Laboratory results validate the simulations and confirm the pyramid quality. On sky, stable closed-loop operation was achieved over three nights. Compared with the VIS-mPWFS at 500 Hz, the IR-umPWFS at 1000 Hz showed gains of ~1.63 in Strehl ratio and ~1.26 in encircled energy, and a ~2.03-fold reduction in residual wavefront error. The IR-umPWFS remained robust to tip-tilt offsets up to 3 lambda/D at 1400 nm. It is a strong candidate for future XAO systems: operating in the infrared extends the linear range sufficiently to remove the need for modulation, enabling higher loop speeds no longer limited by the modulation mirror, which contributed to the improved on-sky performance. The OHP turbulence (D/r0 ~ 17 at 1400 nm) is comparable to typical VLT conditions (D/r0 ~ 20), supporting the relevance of these results for VLT/RISTRETTO.

Abstract: Space-based astrometric missions such as Hipparcos and Gaia rely on one-dimensional scanning strategies that couple the measured signals with both observation times and scanning geometry. This coupling can introduce spurious periodicities and complicate their interpretation. We present a non-parametric extension of the phase-distance correlation (PDC) periodogram that quantifies and reduces dependence on scan geometry at the period-search stage. Using Hipparcos data, we identified recurrent periodicities associated with characteristic scanning-law timescales and showed that they are strongly suppressed by the scan-angle-controlled statistic. We then applied the method to Gaia Data Release 4 (DR4) prerelease epoch astrometry, where a recurrent ~54-day feature is suppressed across multiple sources while independently known orbital signals remain detectable. The method provides a diagnostic of scan-angle dependence and a way to mitigate its impact on period searches. These results are particularly relevant for Gaia DR4, where public epoch astrometry will enable large-scale searches for companions ranging from exoplanets to dormant black holes.

Abstract: We generalize a previous formulation for the estimation of polarization cross-talk induced by atmospheric seeing to the case of image jitter that must be described as a non-stationary, and generally non-isotropic, random process. We demonstrate how the newly derived formalism converges towards the previous one in the limit of stationary random image jitter. In the case of non-stationary processes, we compare the predictions of the two formalisms, and illustrate the salient differences. For the sake of demonstration, we adopt for our analysis a specific example of power-spectral density derived from a circulant auto-correlation function, which describes a class of stationary random processes periodic over the length of the time series, and which we successively modified by the insertion of off-diagonal power spectral features at different frequencies in order to test the extended modeling framework for the estimation of polarization cross-talk.

Abstract: Thermal radiation transport (TRT) is computationally challenging because the photon specific intensity depends simultaneously on space, angle, and frequency, producing a high-dimensional state space whose cost grows rapidly with resolution. Tensor-network methods, such as the tensor train (TT) decomposition, can mitigate this curse of dimensionality when the solution admits low-rank structure, but localized nonseparable structure can drive substantial rank growth. Adaptive mesh refinement (AMR) provides a complementary means of making high spatial resolution tractable by concentrating resolution on features of interest that would otherwise be prohibitively expensive to resolve. When tensor representations are constructed locally on AMR blocks, this decomposition offers an additional advantage: nonseparable structure can remain confined to the blocks in which it occurs rather than driving rank growth throughout the domain. This paper introduces a framework for combining block-adaptive mesh refinement with block-local tensor-train representations of the photon specific intensity. The approach couples spatial adaptivity with localized low-rank compression, allowing fine-scale features to be resolved while limiting the impact of locally complex structure on tensor ranks elsewhere in the domain. We develop conservative ghost-cell transfer, coarse-fine flux correction, and refinement criteria that balance spatial discretization and tensor-rounding errors. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the method preserves low-rank structure across much of the domain while resolving localized features with AMR, achieving compression factors exceeding $\sim$10$^3$. The resulting framework provides a practical foundation for applying tensor-network methods to high-dimensional TRT on the multiscale, adaptively refined meshes encountered in radiation hydrodynamics simulations.

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the recent observation that maximizing flavor entanglement in Higgs boson scattering in a two Higgs doublet model leads to parameter space where the potential, if bounded from below, has an enhanced mirror symmetry $U(2)^2$. Looking at $N$ Higgs doublet models, we find a generalization of the mirror models, with $U(2)^N$ symmetry. Focusing on three Higgs doublet models, we determine that there is also a bounded potential solution that maximally entangles but has only the minimal $U(2)$ symmetry. This counterexample demonstrates that additional constraints must be imposed to maintain the connection between maximal entanglement and extended symmetries. As a potential constraint, for the three Higgs doublet model, we show that requiring maximal entanglement still occurs after gauging the symmetries eliminates the counterexample, thus restoring the connection.

Abstract: Asymmetric dark matter may form self-gravitating macroscopic structures called dark stars if its microscopic properties give rise to sufficient effective pressure to counteract gravity. Consequently, extended dark star configurations can produce distinct observational signatures even when their outer regions have little effect on the intrinsic baryonic stellar structure, providing a potential probe of otherwise hidden dark structures.

Abstract: We present a general framework for the description of QED parton showers in hadron decays. The formalism is constructed in terms of coherent QED antennae with full dependence on particle masses and extends beyond the YFS approach by systematically incorporating spin-dependent (quasi)collinear single-unresolved limits to all orders. In addition, we incorporate corrections derived from tree-level matrix elements up to $\mathcal{O}(α^2)$ and describe a generic procedure to obtain such matrix elements using FeynRules and MadGraph. We provide an explicit implementation of the method for decays involving pseudoscalar mesons and for decays of vectors and pseudoscalars into fermion-antifermion pairs, within the combined Pythia+Vincia parton-shower Monte Carlo framework, and compare the resulting QED emission spectra to those generated with Photos and Sherpa+Photons++ through the EvtGen generator.

</details>


### [90] [Natural Dark Matter in Generalized No-scale $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$ at the HL-LHC and LUX-ZEPLIN Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07385)
*Tianjun Li,James A. Maxin,Dimitri V. Nanopoulos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在GUT模型F-SU(5)与广义No-Scale超引力结合框架下，亚200 GeV中性子和右手中微子是否仍可行；结果表明该模型可自然解决电弱精细调节问题，并提供符合Planck观测的暗物质候选者，且相关参数空间可在LUX-ZEPLIN和HL-LHC实验中被检验。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验轻质量中性子和右手中微子在超对称大统一模型中是否仍可行，以回应长期因实验限制而被排除的传统‘bulk区域’设想，并满足高精度宇宙学暗物质丰度测量要求。

Method: 将F-SU(5)大统一模型（含TeV尺度矢量样多重态flippons）与广义No-Scale超引力（有效超自然SUSY）相结合，构建理论框架；系统分析其参数空间，兼容LHC Run 3胶子/标量夸克限制、Planck暗物质观测及未来LUX-ZEPLIN与HL-LHC探测灵敏度。

Conclusion: F-SU(5)与广义No-Scale SUGRA的结合不仅复活了传统bulk区域的物理可行性，还自然解释暗物质并缓解电弱精细调节问题；LUX-ZEPLIN（2026–27）与HL-LHC（2030起）将联合验证该模型。

Abstract: Is a supersymmetric bulk region with sub-200 GeV neutralinos and right-handed sleptons still feasible? Initially the bulk was the discovery target of the supersymmetry (SUSY) pioneers in the 1980s, though reality crashed those ambitions over the subsequent years and decades, presumptively excluding these light neutralinos and sleptons, inviting finely tuned dark matter scenarios to satisfy emerging high-precision relic density measurements. The answer to this important question is $yes$, if we consider the GUT model $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$, namely, flipped $SU$(5) supplemented with a string derived TeV-scale extra $10+\overline{10}$ vector-like multiplet and charged vector-like singlet $(XE,XE^c)$, dubbed flippons. We pair $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$ with effective supernatural supersymmetry (SUSY), known as generalized No-Scale Supergravity (SUGRA). This merging of $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$ with generalized No-Scale SUGRA solves the SUSY electroweak fine-tuning problem and includes natural dark matter (NDM) that meets the latest 5$σ$ Planck satellite observations through annihilation only. The NDM in this model is imminently under test by the LUX-ZEPLIN 1000-day experiment, thus prospective observed events by LUX-ZEPLIN in 2026-27 will compel confirmation by the high-luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) energizing in 2030. Therefore, we present the parameter space observable at both LUX-ZEPLIN and the HL-LHC that is consistent with all presently operating experiments, including the LHC Run 3 gluino and squark constraints. Our results predict sub-200 GeV neutralinos and right-handed sleptons in the bulk that are not beyond detection, though more massive electroweakinos greater than 600 GeV are also allowed.

</details>


### [91] [Enhancing Gravitational Waves from Global Strings via a First-Order Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07416)
*Yongtao Jia,Ligong Bian,Huai-Ke Guo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper conducts the first (3+1)-dimensional lattice simulations of a cosmological first-order phase transition involving true-vacuum bubbles and global strings, finding scaling behavior, updated axion dark matter constraints, and enhanced gravitational wave signals detectable by multiple observatories.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To study cosmological first-order phase transitions and their implications for axion dark matter and gravitational wave production, especially in comparison with second-order transitions.

Method: (3+1)-dimensional lattice simulations of a first-order phase transition with nucleating, expanding, and colliding true-vacuum bubbles carrying random phases, leading to global string network formation; analysis of axion spectrum and gravitational wave emission.

Result: String network enters scaling regime with smaller logarithmic growth coefficient than in second-order PT; derived dark-matter relic abundance and BBN-bound on axion parameters $f_a$ and $m_a$; gravitational wave-to-axion emission ratio ~100× larger than second-order case; GW spectrum detectable by pulsar timing arrays, space- and ground-based interferometers.

Conclusion: First-order phase transitions produce distinct, observationally accessible signatures—especially stronger gravitational wave signals—compared to second-order transitions, offering new pathways to probe axion physics and early-universe dynamics.

Abstract: We perform the first (3+1)-dimensional lattice simulations of a cosmological first-order phase transition (PT) in which true-vacuum bubbles with random phases nucleate, expand, and collide, producing a network of global strings. We find that the string network enters the scaling regime, and the coefficient of the logarithmic growth is smaller than the value obtained for a second-order PT. From the axion spectrum, we derive the dark-matter relic abundance and the dark-radiation bound from big bang nucleosynthesis on the axion decay constant $f_a$ and mass $m_a$. The ratio of the gravitational wave emission rate to the axion emission rate is found to be about two orders of magnitude larger than in the second-order case, and the resulting gravitational wave spectrum can be probed by pulsar timing arrays as well as by space- and ground-based interferometers.

</details>


### [92] [Optimal-Transport Contrastive Learning for Centrality Determination in Heavy-Ion Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07465)
*Shu-Yun Yang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优传输的无监督方法来确定重离子碰撞的中心度，通过Wasserstein距离和点云网络嵌入提升中心度分辨能力，但在实际运动学截断下性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度确定重离子碰撞中心度对逐事件分析（如净质子涨落测量以定位QCD临界点）至关重要；现有有监督机器学习方法依赖模型生成的标签，存在模型依赖性。

Method: 采用无监督最优传输方法：用1-Wasserstein距离表征每个碰撞事件到一组覆盖多重数范围的标志性事件的距离；再用对比学习训练的点云网络压缩距离为紧凑事件嵌入；最后按嵌入空间均匀划分中心度等级。

Result: 该方法比传统基于粒子多重数截断的方法具有更优的中心度分辨率（以N_part分布归一化宽度衡量），且能更好聚类具有相似N_part的事件；但在施加真实运动学截断后性能显著下降，归因于中心与外围碰撞特征区分度降低。

Conclusion: 基于最优传输的无监督中心度判定方法在理想条件下有效，但应用于实验数据仍面临挑战，尤其在真实探测器接受度限制下，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Determining centrality of heavy-ion collisions with high resolution is essential for event-by-event analyses, in particular for net-proton fluctuation measurements aimed at locating the QCD critical point. Supervised machine-learning approaches rely on labels generated by model and therefore inherit the model dependence of the training target. In this work, we propose an unsupervised method to determine collision centrality based on optimal transport. Each collision is represented by its 1-Wasserstein distances to a fixed set of landmark events chosen to cover the measured multiplicity range. A point-cloud network trained by contrastive learning then compresses these distances into a compact event embedding, which is partitioned into equal-size centrality classes. We find that this approach groups events with similar $N_{\rm part}$ and yields a better centrality resolution, quantified by the normalized width of the $N_{\rm part}$ distribution, than the conventional method of cutting on particle multiplicity. The method, however, encounters a bottleneck once realistic kinematic cuts are applied to the particles. We attribute this degraded performance to the loss of distinct features between central and peripheral collisions because of applying kinematic cuts. The application of optimal transport to centrality determination in experimental data, remains an open problem that needs further study.

</details>

*Mojtaba Hosseini,Rouzbeh Rouzbehi*
