<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解释暗物质本质并解决标准模型中电弱能标自然性问题，构建经典尺度不变的扩展模型，同时实现多组分稳定暗物质和电弱对称性自发破缺。

Method: 引入含复标量S、两个狄拉克费米子ψ¹,²和U(1)ₐ规范玻色子V_μ的隐藏扇区，S通过Higgs门户与SM耦合；分析参数空间中三粒子稳定性；结合宇宙学观测（暗物质丰度）、直接探测实验约束及有限温有效势计算，研究电弱相变性质并预言引力波谱。

Result: 确定了满足暗物质丰度与当前直接探测限制的参数区域；发现存在一阶电弱相变，并计算其引力波谱，该谱处于LISA等未来空间干涉仪的可探测范围内。

Conclusion: 该经典尺度不变三组分暗物质模型虽参数空间受限，但可通过下一代引力波实验进行关键检验，为暗物质与早期宇宙相变研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study a classically scale-invariant extension of the Standard Model (SM) with four new fields in a hidden sector: a complex scalar $S$, two Dirac fermions $ψ^{1,2}$ and the gauge boson $V_μ$ of a new $U(1)_D$ symmetry. The scalar $S$ couples to the SM through the Higgs portal. We identify regions of parameter space in which $V_μ$, $ψ^1$ and $ψ^2$ are all stable, giving a three-component dark matter (DM) scenario. The model is classically scale invariant, and the electroweak symmetry breaks because of the loop effects. We impose the observed relic density and current direct detection bounds, and then study the finite temperature effective potential. For a benchmark point, we find a first-order phase transition and compute the resulting gravitational wave (GW) spectrum, which we compare with the projected sensitivities of space-based interferometers. Although the viable parameter space is small, it can be probed by future experiments.

</details>


### [94] [Power corrections to heavy-quark spin correlations in small-$x$ deep-inelastic scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07518)
*Meisen Gao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了小-x深度非弹性散射中重夸克自旋关联在背对背极限之外的修正，计算了有限夸克质量和有限Nc下均匀高斯靶的树图级首阶非零修正，并分析了自旋关联对不同靶模型及测量设置的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克自旋关联在小-x区域超出背对背近似时的行为尚不明确，且其靶依赖性与动力学修正需定量刻画，以厘清QCD演化中自旋-动量耦合的本质。

Method: 在旋转对称靶假设下，通过全方位角平均消去靶依赖；采用树图级微扰计算，保留有限夸克质量与有限Nc；展开至偶极尺寸四阶，包含对数项、接触项与干涉项；数值积分检验高斯核未展开结果；结合TMD改进近似进行对比；并基于联合自旋测量与似然比分析评估信息增益。

Result: 1）靶依赖在归一化自旋密度矩阵中于方位角平均后消失；2）四阶修正主导行为为m ln P / P^3；3）两种高斯靶在全部12组运动学比较中自旋关联均不同（仅一组仍可分）；4）理想横向光子+三动量窄窗+共轴自旋测量下，自旋信息使拟合后对数似然比提升3.5–3.7%。

Conclusion: 重夸克自旋关联存在显著的模型依赖性修正，其大小与形式受靶结构、夸克质量及测量构型共同调控；该效应属理论可计算的模型依赖现象，而非直接实验灵敏度预言。

Abstract: Heavy-quark spin correlations in small-$x$ deep-inelastic scattering receive corrections beyond the back-to-back limit. For a rotationally symmetric target at leading power, the target dependence cancels from the normalized spin density matrix after full azimuthal averaging, for fixed kinematics and spin axes related by transverse light-front boosts. We calculate the first nonvanishing correction at tree level for finite quark mass and finite $N_c$ in a homogeneous Gaussian target. The expansion through fourth order in the dipole sizes includes logarithms, contact terms and all interference contributions at that order. Comparison with the improved transverse-momentum-dependent approximation separates kinematic corrections from terms with additional insertions of the target current. Numerical integration of the unexpanded Gaussian kernel tests the approximation at 28 parameter points. Spin correlations differ between the two targets in all 12 kinematic comparisons; in one comparison both spin states remain separable. At fixed photon virtuality, the leading large-$P$ behavior of the fourth-order correction to the normalized transverse spin state is generically proportional to $m\ln P/P^3$. We also compare two fixed Gaussian targets using unpolarized momentum distributions and joint spin measurements. For a purely transverse photon, three narrow momentum bins and an ideal measurement of both spins along the common quark-momentum axis defined by the light-front boost, spin information adds 3.5-3.7 percent to the expected log likelihood ratio after the overall normalization is fitted. This increase depends on the spin analyzing power and bin selection. The calculation establishes a model-dependent effect, rather than an experimental sensitivity prediction.

</details>


### [95] [Can cosmic neutrinos induce a cosmic birefringence signal?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07578)
*Mario Reig*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了光子与宇宙中微子不对称电流耦合以解释CMB偏振各向同性旋转的可能机制，指出该机制在规范不变UV完备性下需引入与无质量轴子严格对偶的Kalb-Ramond场，并导出耦合参数关系；但该参数被电子磁矩、光穿墙实验及恒星演化等观测强烈排除，故宇宙中微子无法产生可观测的双折射信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解释宇宙微波背景（CMB）偏振观测中提示的各向同性旋转现象，提出光子与宇宙中微子不对称电流耦合的机制。

Method: 基于规范不变UV完备性要求，引入Kalb-Ramond场并利用其与无质量轴子的严格对偶性；结合SU(2)_L规范对称性推导轴子-光子与轴子-电子耦合关系，并对比现有实验与天体物理约束。

Result: 轴子-光子与轴子-中微子电流耦合乘积被固定为g_{aγ}/f_ℓ = 1/Λ_*²（Λ_* ≈ 10 TeV），进而推出轴子-电子耦合g_{aγ}g_{ae} ≈ 5×10⁻¹² GeV⁻¹，该值被电子反常磁矩、光穿墙实验和恒星演化观测排除；若引入反常自由守恒流，则模型或被SM费米子接触相互作用排除，或使遗迹中微子脱离信号。

Conclusion: 宇宙中微子无法诱导可观测的CMB偏振双折射信号。

Abstract: A coupling of photons to the cosmic neutrino asymmetry current has been proposed to explain the hinted isotropic rotation of CMB polarization. Gauge invariant UV completions require a Kalb-Ramond field that is exactly dual to a massless axion. We show that, in this case, the product of the axion-photon coupling and the axion-neutrino current coupling is fixed by the signal, $g_{aγ}/f_\ell=1/Λ_*^2$ with $Λ_*\simeq10$ TeV. Using $SU(2)_L$ gauge invariance we show that the dual axion couples to electrons as $g_{aγ}g_{ae}=m_e/Λ_*^2\simeq5\times10^{-12}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. This value is excluded by the measured electron magnetic moment combined with light-shining-through-a-wall searches, as well as by stellar evolution. Conserved anomaly-free currents may evade these bounds, but then either a contact interaction among SM fermions excludes the model, or the relic neutrinos decouple from the signal. We conclude that cosmic neutrinos cannot induce a birefringence signal.

</details>


### [96] [Estimating Spectrum-Free Information Bounds for Generative Surrogates Beyond Conditional Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07826)
*Chaitanya Dumbre,Sourabh Dube*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出在高能物理中，仅用1D边缘散度评估条件生成代理模型（如从部分子预测喷注）可能导致条件坍缩问题；作者改用预报验证中的连续排序概率技巧得分（CRPSS）进行更稳健评估，并提出基于k近邻的数据驱动外推法估计条件信息所能达到的最佳CRPS，发现现有模型（MDN和CFM）已逼近该上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统1D边缘散度指标易奖励条件坍缩，无法可靠区分模型对条件信息的有效利用程度，需更鲁棒的评估方法。

Method: 采用连续排序概率技能得分（CRPSS）替代KL和Wasserstein-1等边缘散度；引入数据驱动的k-最近邻（k-NN）外推法估计条件信息所能支持的最佳CRPS；并在不同条件变量区间内分段评估全局技能。

Result: 在CMS模拟基准上，忽略条件变量的采样器竟优于训练好的MDN；CFM与之在部分目标上难以区分；而CRPSS评估显示两个模型均与k-NN估计的最佳CRPS高度一致。

Conclusion: 当前主流条件生成模型（MDN、CFM）在所选四个目标变量上的表现已接近条件输入所能提供的信息上限，表明其条件建模能力已达瓶颈，需新评估范式与更强条件表达能力。

Abstract: Conditional generative surrogates in high-energy physics, such as models that predict reconstructed jets from partons, are often evaluated on $1\text{D}$ marginal divergences among other metrics. Taken alone these metrics can reward conditional collapse. On a CMS simulation benchmark, a sampler that ignores the conditioner beats a trained Mixture Density Network (MDN) on all four target marginals under both KL and Wasserstein-1, and cannot be stably separated from a Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) model on two of the four. We instead score the models with the Continuous Ranked Probability Skill Score (CRPSS) from forecast verification, taking the exact marginal as the reference. We show how global skill depends on the evaluation spectrum, and we also report it within bins of a conditioning variable, where that dependence largely goes away. We then introduce a data-driven $k$-Nearest-Neighbor ($k$-NN) extrapolation that estimates the best CRPS the conditioning information allows. Both models agree with this estimate on all four targets, which suggests that current architectures have reached the information available in the conditioner, at least as far as these per-target scores can tell.

</details>


### [97] [Long lived MeV scale bound state dark matter alleviates the ${}^7 Li$ tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07852)
*Pa. Gokhula Prasad,V. Suryanarayana Mummidi*

TL;DR: 本文研究了MeV尺度不稳定束缚态暗物质对热和非热大爆炸核合成的影响，发现长寿命束缚态（寿命约1-100秒）可缓解⁷Li丰度问题，且结合非热核合成可同时满足其他轻元素观测约束。

Motivation: 解决标准大爆炸核合成中长期存在的⁷Li丰度观测值与理论预测值之间的矛盾（⁷Li问题），并检验不稳定束缚态暗物质模型在核合成历史中的自洽性。

Result: 长寿命束缚态（τ ≈ 1–100 s）结合非热核合成可在缓解⁷Li问题的同时，保持D、⁴He等其他轻元素丰度与观测一致；非热效应被证实对此类暗物质模型的核合成一致性至关重要。

Conclusion: 不稳定束缚态暗物质（尤其是MeV尺度、寿命~1–100秒的长寿命态）通过耦合非热核合成，可协调解决⁷Li问题且不破坏其他轻元素丰度约束，凸显非热过程在早期宇宙核合成中的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of MeV scale unstable bound state dark matter on both thermal and non thermal Big Bang nucleosynthesis. Focusing on the weakly self interacting regime, we find that this framework can reproduce the theoretically predicted abundances through both short and long lived bound states. While long lived bound states can alleviate the cosmological ${}^7$Li problem, they simultaneously lead to discrepancies with other light element abundances. We show that this tension is resolved by incorporating non thermal nucleosynthesis, which act on these non standard initial light element abundances to alleviate the ${}^7 Li$ tension without violating observational constraints on the other elements. Remarkably, across the range of $\widetildeρ_B$ and $m_χ$ considered, long lived bound states with lifetimes $10^0\,{\rm s}\lesssimτ\lesssim10^2\,{\rm s}$ can simultaneously resolve the ${}^7$Li problem and a large parameter space remain consistent with the observed abundances of the other light elements. Our results demonstrate that non thermal effects are essential for obtaining a consistent nucleosynthesis history in unstable bound state dark matter scenarios.

</details>


### [98] [Phenomenological investigation of order statistics for multi-jet events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07838)
*S. Cerci,G. Chachamis,A. Sabio Vera,Z. S. Demiroglu,D. Sunar Cerci,D. Vaccaro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了质子-质子碰撞中按等级排序的喷注快度分布，作为多喷注辐射的探针，并比较了BFKL理论模型（BFKLex）与传统部分子碎裂模型（PYTHIA 8.306）的预测能力，验证其在高能QCD辐射研究中的实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单喷注快度谱无法反映事件内喷注的空间组织信息，因此需引入秩序快度分布以更精细探测多喷注辐射机制。

Method: 基于13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据，计算并比较BFKLex（BFKL框架）和PYTHIA 8.306（共线部分子淋浴）对固定三喷注构型及跨多重数秩序可观测量的归一化预测；评估PDF、调谐参数、因子化/重整化尺度等系统不确定性；采用总变差距离和Wasserstein距离量化模型差异。

Result: 在多个高统计区域，BFKLex与PYTHIA的预测差异显著超出各自不确定性带；极值喷注间最小快度间隔以秩依赖方式影响可观测量分辨能力。

Conclusion: 秩序喷注快度谱是实验上可行的可观测量，可作为传统Mueller-Navelet测量的有力补充，用于深入研究高能QCD辐射动力学。

Abstract: We investigate rank-ordered jet rapidity distributions as probes of multijet radiation in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV. These observables retain information about the organization of jets within an event that is not resolved by the inclusive single-jet rapidity spectrum. We compare normalized predictions from the BFKL-based generator BFKLex and the collinear parton-shower generator PYTHIA 8.306, considering both fixed three-jet configurations and rank observables defined across multiplicities. We assess PDF and tune variations for PYTHIA 8.306, and PDF and factorization- and renormalization-scale variations for BFKLex. Total-variation and Wasserstein distances quantify the separation between the baseline shapes. Differences remain visible relative to the evaluated uncertainty bands in several well-populated regions. A minimum rapidity separation between the extremal jets modifies the discriminating power of the observables in a rank-dependent way. These results support rank-ordered rapidity spectra as experimentally accessible observables complementary to conventional Mueller-Navelet measurements for investigating high-energy QCD radiation.

</details>


### [99] [The Colour Glass Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07842)
*Edmond Iancu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文全面介绍了色玻璃凝聚体（CGC）有效理论，该理论是高能量子色动力学（QCD）中处理高部分子密度下强子相互作用的第一性原理框架，重点阐述了胶子饱和、横动量展宽和几何标度等物理图像与现象，并介绍了其核心形式：半经典近似、非线性JIMWLK方程及其平均场近似BK方程；还通过电子-核子、质子-核子碰撞等稀疏-致密过程展示了该理论的应用，并简要讨论了高阶辐射修正、JIMWLK的Langevin表述及CGC中横动量依赖（TMD）因子化的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一个能描述高能QCD中大密度部分子系统（特别是胶子饱和）的第一性原理有效理论框架。

Method: 基于色玻璃凝聚体（CGC）有效理论，采用半经典近似刻画致密胶子系统，利用非线性JIMWLK方程描述多胶子关联的能量演化，并引入其平均场近似BK方程；通过‘稀疏-致密’散射过程（如e-A、p-A碰撞）进行应用演示。

Result: 系统阐明了CGC理论的物理图像（如胶子饱和、几何标度、横动量展宽），建立了其核心形式体系，并展示了在实际高能碰撞中的适用性；同时指出其可自然导出TMD因子化，并支持向更高阶修正和随机动力学（Langevin）表述拓展。

Conclusion: CGC是理解高能强子碰撞中饱和物理的关键有效理论，兼具坚实QCD基础与丰富现象学内涵，为未来高能核物理研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive introduction to the Colour Glass Condensate (CGC) effective theory, a first-principles framework for hadronic interactions in high-energy Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) at large parton densities. The focus is on the underlying physical picture and associated emergent phenomena, such as gluon saturation, transverse momentum broadening, and geometric scaling. We introduce the key components of the CGC formalism: the semiclassical approximation for dense gluon systems at saturation, the nonlinear JIMWLK equation governing the energy evolution of multi-gluon correlations, and its mean-field approximation, the BK equation. The formalism is illustrated through applications to selected "dilute-dense" processes, namely high-energy electron-nucleus and proton-nucleus collisions. Finally, we briefly discuss advanced topics including higher-order radiative corrections, the Langevin formulation of the JIMWLK equation, and the emergence of transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) factorisation from the CGC effective theory.

</details>


### [100] [Generalized parton distributions of the nucleon in the chiral quark soliton model II: Chiral anomaly mediation in the polarized GPDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07869)
*Josuke Minamiguchi,Tomoya Uji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在手征夸克孤子模型中计算了非零偏斜度和动量转移下的极化味单态广义部分子分布（GPDs）$\widetilde H^{u+d}$ 和 $\widetilde E^{u+d}$，重点研究了由手征反常产生的单态赝标量介子 $η_0$ 对结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解手征反常如何通过单态介子 $η_0$ 影响核子的极化广义部分子分布，特别是其在ERBL区域的行为特征。

Method: 采用手征夸克孤子模型，在包含和不包含单态赝标介子 $η_0$ 的两种情形下，计算极化味单态GPDs $\widetilde H^{u+d}$ 和 $\widetilde E^{u+d}$；分析 $η_0$ 质量（由手征反常诱导）对结果的影响，并引入显式介子交换项解释物理机制。

Result: $η_0$ 对 $\widetilde E^{u+d}$ 在ERBL区有显著贡献，但对 $\widetilde H^{u+d}$ 仅微小修正；介子交换项可定性解释该差异并呈现预期的单态极点；当反常质量设为零时，极点变为无质量，交换项在小动量转移下主导 $\widetilde E^{u+d}$。

Conclusion: 手征反常诱导的单态介子动力学在核子极化GPDs中留下清晰可观测的特征，尤其体现在 $\widetilde E^{u+d}$ 的ERBL区域行为上。

Abstract: We calculate the polarized flavor-singlet generalized parton distributions (GPDs) $\widetilde H^{u+d}$ and $\widetilde E^{u+d}$ in the chiral quark soliton model at nonzero skewness and momentum transfer. Results are obtained with and without a singlet pseudoscalar meson $η_0$, whose mass is generated by the chiral anomaly. Relative to the pion-only model, the singlet field introduces a sizable contribution to $\widetilde E^{u+d}$ in the ERBL region but only a minor correction to $\widetilde H^{u+d}$. An explicit meson-exchange term qualitatively accounts for this contrast and displays the anticipated singlet pole. When the anomaly-induced mass is set to zero, the pole becomes massless, and the exchange term dominates $\widetilde E^{u+d}$ at small momentum transfer. These findings demonstrate how anomalous singlet-meson dynamics leaves distinct signatures in the polarized GPDs of the nucleon.

</details>


### [101] [Can Two-Point Correlations Determine Gravitational Waves from Sound Waves?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07877)
*Guangshang Chen,Jianpeng Dai,Yang Xiao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了宇宙一级相变中声波作为引力波主要源时，速度两点关联函数是否足以唯一确定引力波谱的问题；通过解析示例和真实声源模型分析，表明四点关联等高阶效应在声学传播阶段通常可忽略，但宇宙膨胀会削弱这种压制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上使用流体速度两点关联函数（高斯近似）预测相变产生的引力波谱，但引力波源是速度的二次函数，因此速度的连通四点关联可能也有贡献，需检验两点关联是否足够。

Method: 采用两个解析的玩具模型进行理论论证，并构建基于随机气泡成核与生长的真实声源模型，结合线性声学传播和宇宙膨胀效应进行分析。

Result: 两点关联不能总是唯一确定引力波谱；在真实声源的后续线性声学传播中，连通四点关联贡献是次主导的，但宇宙膨胀限制了该压制效果。

Conclusion: 两点关联重构引力波谱在多数声学阶段是可靠的，但需考虑高阶速度关联及声源持续时间对引力波预测的影响。

Abstract: Sound waves are expected to be a dominant source of gravitational waves (GWs) from cosmological first-order phase transitions. Their spectrum is commonly predicted using the fluid velocity two-point correlation function under the Gaussian approximation. However, since the GW source is quadratic in velocity, connected four-point correlations may also contribute to the GW spectrum. Through two analytical toy examples, we demonstrate that velocity two-point correlations alone does not always uniquely determine the GW spectrum. Moving beyond these idealized examples, we investigate a realistic acoustic source based on stochastic bubble nucleation and growth, proving that the connected contribution becomes subleading during subsequent linear acoustic propagation, although cosmic expansion limits this suppression. These results clarify when two-point reconstruction is reliable and how higher-order velocity correlations and the duration of acoustic emission affect GW predictions.

</details>


### [102] [Investigating bottom quark effects in Higgs boson pair production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08183)
*David Bürg,Gudrun Heinrich,Philipp Rendler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究底夸克质量对希格斯玻色子对产生过程的影响，发现其在总截面层面可忽略，但在双希格斯不变质量分布接近产生阈值时有显著效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究底夸克质量在希格斯玻色子对产生中的不同作用，包括底夸克圈图和初态底夸克的贡献。

Method: 分析底夸克圈图与初态底夸克对希格斯玻色子对产生的影响，重点关注总截面与不变质量分布（尤其是阈值附近）的行为。

Result: 底夸克质量效应在总截面层面可忽略，但在双希格斯不变质量分布接近产生阈值时呈现明显影响。

Conclusion: 底夸克质量在阈值区域对双希格斯产生动力学具有不可忽略的作用，需在精确理论预测中予以考虑。

Abstract: We investigate different effects of bottom quark masses in Higgs boson pair production, considering both bottom quark loops and bottom quarks in the initial state. While the effects are negligible at total cross section level, the situation is different for the Higgs boson pair invariant mass distribution close to the production threshold of the two Higgs bosons.

</details>


### [103] [Light Primordial Black Holes from a First-Order Phase Transition near the End of Inflation: Baryogenesis and Gravitational Wave Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08187)
*Zihong Cheng,Fa Peng Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了暴胀期间旁观者场一级相变中真空泡成核所形成的轻原初黑洞（PBHs）及其宇宙学后果，提出了一种旁观者势随暴胀场滚动演化的唯象模型，并探讨了PBH形成、稳定性、蒸发引发的电弱对称性恢复与重子不对称性生成，以及相关引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轻原初黑洞（PBHs）的新形成机制及其在重子不对称性生成和引力波探测中的潜在可观测效应，弥补标准热大爆炸模型在这些方面的不足。

Method: 构建一个旁观者场势随暴胀场缓慢滚动而演化的唯象模型，模拟真空泡在暴胀期间成核、生长及真空序反转过程；分析泡内假真空态的稳定性（抑制反向隧穿）；计算超临界泡演化为PBH的概率与质量分布；评估PBH蒸发导致局域电弱对称性恢复及重子数产生；并计算霍金辐射直接产生的引力波及PBH密度扰动诱导的引力波。

Result: 轻PBH蒸发可在大爆炸核合成前局域恢复电弱对称性，结合CP破坏可产生达观测值10%量级的重子不对称性；诱导引力波信号峰值幅度可达Ω_GW,0 h² ∼ 10⁻⁷，频率约10–10² Hz。

Conclusion: 该模型为轻PBH提供了一种可行的暴胀期形成机制，并自然关联重子生成与高频引力波信号，具备可观测前景，为检验早期宇宙新物理提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the formation and cosmological consequences of light primordial black holes (PBHs) from vacuum bubbles nucleated during a spectator sector first-order phase transition in inflation. We construct a phenomenological model in which the spectator potential evolves with the rolling inflaton, allowing vacuum bubbles to nucleate and grow during inflation. The vacuum ordering subsequently reverses near the end of inflation, leaving the bubble interiors in a metastable false vacuum state. We examine their stability against reverse vacuum tunneling and impose benchmark conditions to suppress this process before and after reheating. Sufficiently large bubbles can subsequently undergo supercritical evolution and form PBHs. In this framework, the PBH abundance is determined by the vacuum tunneling history, while the characteristic mass depends on the nucleation epoch and bubble growth during inflation. For sufficiently light PBHs that evaporate before big bang nucleosynthesis, Hawking radiation can create locally superheated regions in which electroweak symmetry is restored, potentially generating a baryon asymmetry in the presence of additional CP-violating interactions. We also investigate the direct gravitational wave (GW) signals from Hawking radiation and the induced GWs from PBH density fluctuations, the latter arising if an early PBH dominated epoch occurs. For representative benchmark parameters, the resulting baryon asymmetry can reach the ten-percent level of the observed value, while the induced GW signal can reach a peak amplitude of $Ω_{\rm GW,0}h^2\sim10^{-7}$ at frequencies of order $10$-$10^2\,{\rm Hz}$.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum Fourier integration of decay rates at next-to-leading order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08204)
*David F. Rentería-Estrada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用量子神经网络（QNN）结合Loop-Tree Duality（LTD）框架，在高能物理中实现NLO精度衰变率积分的新方法，通过傅里叶级数表征和量子振幅估计完成数值积分，并在多个过程上验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中NLO精度的衰变率计算涉及复杂且发散的实虚部积分，传统数值方法计算代价高；亟需更高效、可扩展的量子计算方案。

Method: 采用数据编码量子电路构建QNN，利用其天然的截断傅里叶级数表示能力逼近LTD给出的局部有限被积函数；再结合量子傅里叶迭代振幅估计（QFIAE）对系数进行积分；在H→qq̄(g)、γ*→qq̄(g)、Φ→φφ(φ)等过程中开展模拟器与硬件实验。

Result: QNN成功逼近目标被积函数，QFIAE给出的积分结果与解析NLO预言在误差范围内一致，验证了该傅里叶基QNN积分方案在量子模拟器和真实量子硬件上的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于傅里叶表示的QNN积分是处理高能物理NLO计算的一种有前景的量子算法路径，为未来在容错量子计算机上执行复杂微扰计算提供了新范式。

Abstract: We discuss the use of quantum neural networks (QNNs) as a tool for integrating decay rates at next-to-leading order (NLO) in perturbation theory. Data-encoding quantum circuits admit a truncated Fourier-series representation, enabling a trained QNN to approximate a target integrand and provide the coefficients subsequently integrated with Quantum Fourier Iterative Amplitude Estimation (QFIAE). The causal-unitary formulation of the Loop-Tree Duality (LTD) combines virtual and real contributions into locally finite integrands suitable for this representation. We consider $H\to q\bar q(g)$, $γ^*\to q\bar q(g)$ and $Φ\toφφ(φ)$, comparing quantum-simulator and quantum-hardware results with analytic NLO predictions. The study illustrates the potential of Fourier-based QNN integration for high-energy physics.

</details>


### [105] [DIS-secting the universe with high-energy neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08221)
*Rhorry Gauld,Lorenzo Guerra,Ulrich Haisch,Alessio Vorona*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了电弱辐射修正对中微子诱导的带电流深度非弹性散射（CC DIS）中带电轻子运动学分布的影响，特别是对高能μ子能量谱的影响；作者在蒙特卡洛框架中实现了完整的NLO电弱修正，并结合高阶对数增强的末态QED辐射和相干散射贡献；与POWHEG-BOX生成器的NLO QCD+部分子碎裂结果进行了比较，给出了实验分析中使用该生成器的建议；发现辐射修正可达10%以上，在~10⁴ GeV以上时，光子引发过程和相干W玻色子产生有显著贡献，但现有生成器未包含这些效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弱辐射修正对中微子望远镜中高能μ子能量谱等观测量具有重要影响，尤其在高能区传统QCD模拟可能遗漏关键电弱效应，需系统评估其影响并指导实验模拟工具的正确使用。

Method: 在灵活的蒙特卡洛框架中实现CC DIS的完整NLO电弱修正，补充高阶对数增强的末态QED辐射和相干散射；与基于POWHEG-BOX的NLO QCD+部分子碎裂模拟进行多模型对比；评估修正对μ子能谱、μ子–中微子能量关联及平均非弹性度的影响。

Result: 电弱辐射修正可达10%以上；在约10⁴ GeV以上，光子引发过程和相干W玻色子产生贡献显著；现有POWHEG-BOX未包含这些过程，导致高能区模拟偏差；据此提出POWHEG-BOX在实验分析中的使用建议。

Conclusion: 在高能中微子实验（尤其是中微子望远镜）的数据分析中，必须纳入完整的电弱辐射修正，特别是光子引发与相干W产生过程；仅依赖标准QCD+PS模拟（如POWHEG-BOX）将导致系统性偏差，影响能谱重建与物理量提取的精度。

Abstract: We revisit the impact of electroweak (EW) radiative corrections on the kinematic distributions of charged leptons in neutrino-induced charged-current (CC) deep inelastic scattering (DIS). These corrections are particularly relevant for observables sensitive to collinear photon radiation, such as the energy spectrum of high-energy muons detected in neutrino telescopes. To study these effects, we implement full next-to-leading order (NLO) EW corrections to CC DIS in a flexible Monte Carlo framework, supplemented with higher-order logarithmically enhanced final-state QED radiation and coherent scattering contributions. We compare our results to exclusive NLO QCD plus parton shower (PS) predictions obtained with an existing $\texttt{POWHEG-BOX}$ event generator, examining different PS models and settings. Based on this comparison, we provide recommendations for the use of the $\texttt{POWHEG-BOX}$ event generator in experimental analyses. We further assess the impact of radiative corrections on the correlation between the observed muon energy and the incoming neutrino energy, and on the mean inelasticity averaged over a realistic neutrino flux. These corrections can exceed $10\%$, and above about $10^4\,{\rm GeV}$ they receive sizeable contributions from photon-initiated and coherent $W$-boson production, which are not included in the event generator.

</details>


### [106] [Analytic parametrisations of the scale dependence of proton parton distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08272)
*David F. Rentería-Estrada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习辅助拟合的解析形式质子部分子分布函数（ML-PDFs），替代传统插值方法，在精度相当的前提下显著提升蒙特卡洛模拟速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PDF数值计算依赖于对表格化网格的插值，效率较低；需发展高效、高精度且满足物理约束（如求和规则）的解析表示方法。

Method: 将标准x空间参数化中的参数提升为尺度Q²的函数，利用机器学习辅助拟合技术，基于HERAPDF2.0 NLO数据集在10⁻⁴ ≤ x ≤ 0.65及Q²至1 TeV范围内重构系数。

Result: ML-PDFs对多数味夸克积分分布复现精度达亚百分比级（<1%），奇异夸克在最高能标下偏差约1.5%；满足价夸克与动量求和规则（~1%）；在pp→π和pp→γπ截面计算中分别达到约2%和5%精度；相比LHAPDF插值，蒙特卡洛运行时间分别减少11%和30%。

Conclusion: 该解析ML-PDF方案在保持物理一致性与高精度的同时，显著提升计算效率，适用于高精度强子对撞模拟。

Abstract: Parton distribution functions (PDFs) are non-perturbative inputs whose scale dependence is governed by the Dokshitzer-Gribov-Lipatov-Altarelli-Parisi (DGLAP) equations. Their numerical evaluation commonly relies on interpolation of tabulated grids. We discuss an analytic representation of collinear proton PDFs in $(x,Q^2)$, obtained by promoting the parameters of a standard $x$-space parametrisation to functions of the scale. The coefficients are reconstructed from the HERAPDF2.0 NLO set using machine-assisted fitting techniques, with sampling in $10^{-4}\le x\le 0.65$ and scales up to 1 TeV. The resulting ML-PDFs reproduce integrated distributions at the sub-percent level for most flavours, with deviations reaching about 1.5% for the strange quark at the highest scales. They satisfy valence and momentum sum rules at the percent level and reproduce representative $pp\toπ$ and $pp\toγπ$ cross sections within approximately 2% and 5%, respectively, in the kinematic regions studied. Compared with grid interpolation through LHAPDF, direct evaluation of the analytic expressions reduces the total Monte Carlo run time by about 11% and 30% in these two applications.

</details>


### [107] [Algorithmic Construction of Vector-Valued Modular Forms and Application to an $A_4\times\mathbb{Z}_4$-invariant Model of Leptons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08387)
*Q. Bonnefoy,M. Rausch de Traubenberg,V. Saulquin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构造有限模群的向量值模形式（VVMFs）的算法及其实现代码，利用Burnside-Brauer定理系统性地确定生成所有高权VVMFs所需的最小VVMF集合，并自动处理新群研究中的各种技术难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有方法中难以系统性地找到能生成所有高权向量值模形式（VVMFs）的最小初等VVMF集合的问题。

Method: 利用Burnside-Brauer定理识别最小生成集；基于群论与表示论信息，显式构造初等VVMFs；通过张量积生成更高权VVMFs；开发配套自动化代码以处理各类技术细节。

Result: 成功复现了文献中已知有限模群（如A4、S4等）的VVMF结果，并拓展应用于新群如A4×Z4，在轻子模型中验证了该算法的有效性与普适性。

Conclusion: 所提算法和代码为有限模群VVMF的研究提供了系统、通用且自动化的工具，显著提升了模味对称性在粒子物理唯象学中的应用效率与广度。

Abstract: We present an algorithmic procedure that constructs the Vector-Valued Modular Forms (VVMFs) of finite modular groups, as well as its implementation in a companion code. The usual method consists in building the first few VVMFs, whose tensor products then generate the others. We address the difficulty of systematically finding the minimal set of VVMFs that suffices to produce all higher-weight ones, and we propose a method to identify it that makes use of the Burnside-Brauer theorem. For any given group, whose basic group-theoretic and representation-theoretic description is provided by the user, the code determines the minimal set of VVMFs, explicitly builds it using general results of the theory of such functions, then performs the tensor products that generate any other VVMF. Importantly, it automatically takes into account all caveats that might arise during the study of new groups. Our approach reproduces the known results for the finite modular groups explored in the recent phenomenological literature on modular flavor symmetries, and works for additional groups which we tested. We illustrate our algorithm and the working of our code by studying a model of leptons based on the unexplored finite modular group $A_4\times\mathbb{Z}_4$.

</details>


### [108] [Top-Quark Pair Production and Decay in Extended Higgs Sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08388)
*Andrea Banfi,Alexander Lind,Jonas M. Lindert,Vishakha Lingadahally*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的POWHEG-BOX-V2事件生成器（结合OpenLoops 2）用于在NLO精度下模拟含额外重标量与赝标量的扩展Higgs模型中的top夸克对产生及衰变过程，包含完整自旋关联、标量-赝标量干涉效应、QCD本底、强子化与探测器效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决标准模型中电弱相变性质等未解问题，并探索扩展Higgs扇区（如两Higgs双重态模型）在对撞机上的丰富现象学信号。

Method: 改进hvq库以适配POWHEG-BOX-V2与OpenLoops 2联合框架，实现NLO精度的tt̄产生+全自旋关联衰变模拟；显式纳入标量/赝标量信号与SM QCD背景的干涉；并耦合Parton Shower、Hadronisation与Detector Simulation。

Result: 成功实现了在扩展Higgs模型中tt̄过程的高精度模拟，包含干涉效应和实验可观测量建模，为当前及未来对撞机实验提供理论工具支持。

Conclusion: 该工作为探索非标准Higgs扇区（尤其是含重标量与赝标量的模型）提供了首个具备完整NLO+PS+Detector级精度的tt̄模拟框架，具有重要 phenomenological 应用价值。

Abstract: Extensions of the Higgs sector in the Standard Model address outstanding questions like the first-order electroweak phase transition and also present a rich collider phenomenology. The two-Higgs-doublet model is a minimal extension of the Standard Model with an extra complex $SU(2)_L$ doublet, and consists of an extra scalar, a pseudoscalar and a pair of charged Higgs bosons along with a Standard-Model-like Higgs boson. We present a modified version of the hvq library of the POWHEG-BOX-V2 event generator interfaced with the OpenLoops 2 amplitude generator to obtain predictions for top-quark pair production at NLO accuracy, including top-quark decays with full spin correlations in a framework that extends the SM Higgs sector with an extra heavy scalar and a pseudoscalar. The interference effects between the scalar and pseudoscalar signals, and the Standard Model QCD background are implemented. We include parton showers and hadronisation, and take detector effects into consideration. The status of our work and plans to extend it to future collider projects are discussed.

</details>


### [109] [Signatures of Neutrinophilic Pion Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08440)
*Claire Chevallier,Ji-Heng Guo,Giacomo Landini*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于禁闭规范理论的π介子暗物质模型，该模型通过动力学机制同时生成微小的中微子质量和暗物质质量，并探讨了其在解释Super-Kamiokande反电子中微子超出及JUNO探测器检验、以及轻惰性中微子在加速器上位移顶点探测等方面的 phenomenology。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Super-Kamiokande观测到的电子反中微子轻微超出，并为暗物质提供一个与中微子耦合、由规范禁闭动力学生成质量的自洽框架。

Method: 构建一个包含禁闭规范相互作用的理论模型，其中π介子作为暗物质候选者，与中微子耦合；通过计算半湮灭（暗物质+暗物质→暗物质+中微子）和湮灭（暗物质+暗物质→惰性中微子+惰性中微子）过程的热退耦截面，匹配观测的暗物质丰度；结合天体物理与加速器实验约束（如自相互作用、位移顶点等）进行phenomenology分析。

Result: 亚GeV π介子暗物质通过半湮灭产生银河系反中微子通量，可解释Super-Kamiokande反电子中微子超出；JUNO有望在未来验证该解释；另一分支中产生的轻惰性中微子可在加速器实验（如LHC或SHiP）中通过位移顶点信号被探测。

Conclusion: 该模型在满足暗物质丰度、自相互作用等基本约束下，自然关联中微子质量起源与暗物质物理，并提供了可检验的多信使现象学信号。

Abstract: We study the phenomenology of pion dark matter coupled to neutrinos in a confining sector that dynamically generates both small neutrino masses and dark matter mass from gauge confinement. Dark matter semi-annihilation to active neutrinos or annihilation to light sterile neutrinos can reproduce the observed relic abundance through thermal freeze-out while satisfying constraints from self-interactions. In the first scenario, semi-annihilations of sub-GeV dark matter in the galactic halo produce a flux of antineutrinos which can provide a viable interpretation for the mild electron antineutrino excess reported by the Super-Kamiokande collaboration. We assess the potential of the JUNO detector to test this interpretation. In the second scenario, light sterile neutrinos can leave signatures through displaced vertex searches at accelerator facilities.

</details>


### [110] [Testing the Spin of New Physics with Top Precision Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08459)
*André Lessa,Vinícius Rocca,Veronica Sanz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过顶夸克对产生过程中的精确微分测量，研究了三种顶夸克偏好新物理模型（带色标量顶伴随粒子、带色矢量样费米子、自旋一Z′中介子）的动力学区分能力；发现低能有效场论（EFT）无法区分前两者，但在阈值附近利用顶夸克横动量谱可借助其圈图振幅的动量依赖干涉模式实现部分区分，凸显了精密微分测量对虚粒子自旋与动力学的探测潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量不仅能揭示新物理的存在，还能提供其产生机制的动力学信息；需探究不同顶夸克偏好模型在可观测量上的可区分性。

Method: 比较三种顶夸克偏好模型（标量伴子、矢量样费米子、Z′中介子）的贡献机制（圈修正 vs 树图），分析其低能有效算符结构及阈值附近全圈振幅的动量依赖干涉效应，并利用顶夸克横动量谱进行区分性研究。

Result: 标量与矢量样费米子模型在低能EFT下不可区分，但在阈值附近其全圈振幅展现出不同的动量依赖干涉模式；顶夸克pT谱可较易区分共振型（Z′）与圈诱导型效应，而区分标量与矢量样假说虽困难但仍有可测的分布形状差异。

Conclusion: 精密微分测量（如pT谱）能有效探知虚新粒子的自旋与动力学特性，即使其无法被直接重建；结果支持开展更强大的多维联合分析。

Abstract: Precision measurements can reveal not only the presence of new physics, but also information about the dynamics that generated it. We study this question in top-pair production by comparing three top-philic scenarios: a colored scalar top partner, a colored vector-like fermion, and a spin-one $Z^\prime$ mediator. The first two contribute through loop corrections, while the $Z^\prime$ contributes at tree level. We show that the scalar and vector-like fermion scenarios match onto the same effective operator structure at low energies,making their distinction unfeasible using the EFT approach. However, near or above threshold their full loop amplitudes develop distinct momentum-dependent interference patterns. Using the top transverse momentum spectrum we find that resonant and loop-induced effects are relatively easy to distinguish, whereas separating the scalar and vector-like hypotheses is substantially more challenging. Nevertheless, their different analytic structures leave measurable information in the shape of the distribution. These results illustrate how precision differential measurements can probe the spin and dynamics of virtual new states even when they are not directly reconstructed, and motivate more powerful multidimensional analyses.

</details>


### [111] [Joint Bayesian Inference for Extraction of Charmonium Long Distance Matrix Elements Using PYTHIA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08463)
*Yubo Liu,Rongrong Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合贝叶斯方法，同时提取J/ψ、ψ(2S)和χ_c产生过程的非相对论QCD长程矩阵元（LDMEs），利用PYTHIA 8.317中的短程系数与级联衰变结构，基于LHC上286个质子-质子碰撞数据完成拟合，结果χ²/ndf=1.18，并给出满足正定性约束的LDMEs用于PYTHIA输入，还预测了RHIC能区的高横动量J/ψ和ψ(2S)截面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法分别拟合各粲偶素态的LDMEs，忽略其内在关联；本文旨在通过联合拟合，更一致、可靠地提取所有相关LDMEs，并提升对实验数据的整体描述能力。

Method: 采用贝叶斯推断框架，构建通道分辨的响应函数以关联母态产生与子态级联衰变，结合PYTHIA 8.317中实现的NRQCD短距系数和feed-down结构，对LHC在5.02–13 TeV下286个prompt粲偶素测量数据进行全局拟合。

Result: 成功提取出一套整体χ²/ndf=1.18的LDMEs，同时提供满足正定性约束的版本可直接作为PYTHIA输入；并基于该LDMEs预测了√s=200 GeV pp碰撞中高pT的J/ψ和ψ(2S)截面，可供RHIC实验检验。

Conclusion: 联合贝叶斯提取方法显著提升了LDMEs的自洽性和预测能力，为NRQCD机制验证及高能强子对撞中粲偶素产生研究提供了更可靠的理论输入。

Abstract: We present a joint Bayesian extraction of the nonrelativistic QCD long-distance matrix elements (LDMEs) for $J/ψ$, $ψ(2S)$, and $χ_c$ production, using short-distance coefficients and feed-down structure implemented in PYTHIA 8.317. Rather than fitting each charmonium state separately, we construct channel-resolved responses that link parent production to daughter feed-down and infer all LDMEs simultaneously from 286 prompt charmonium measurements in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=5.02$-13 TeV at the LHC. The extracted LDMEs provide a good description of the data, with an overall $χ^2/\mathrm{ndf}=1.18$. We also provide a positivity-constrained set that can be used directly as PYTHIA input. Using the extracted LDMEs, we predict prompt $J/ψ$ and $ψ(2S)$ cross sections at high transverse momentum in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=200$ GeV, which can be tested with RHIC data.

</details>

### [112] [The 2d Ising Model on Hyperbolic Lattices and the Role of Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07045)
*Pallabi Dey,Debasish Banerjee,Arnab Kundu,Ritam Sinha*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了双曲正则镶嵌上铁磁Ising模型临界行为中边界条件的作用，发现开边界下磁化率呈现体积标度律和非平均场指数，而加线边界下表现出平均场行为，并强调了边界条件及体-全域观测量区分对非可均群格点临界现象刻画的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究边界条件如何影响非可均群（如双曲镶嵌）上Ising模型的临界行为，尤其是传统欧氏空间中不适用的有限尺寸标度与普适性问题。

Method: 采用worm算法和Metropolis蒙特卡洛模拟，对比开边界与通过辅助自旋实现的‘加线边界’（wired boundaries），分析磁化率、Binder参数交叉、中心-辅助自旋关联函数、中心自旋对均匀外场响应及层分辨边界场响应等。

Result: 开边界下磁化率呈体积型有限尺寸标度，指数近似稳定且非平均场；加线边界下中心-辅助自旋关联呈现径向平均场磁化指数（δ=3），且内部磁化在弱边界场下被增强。

Conclusion: 边界条件显著改变双曲格点上Ising模型的临界表现；需区分‘内部’与‘全系统’观测量，非可均格点上的普适类可能依赖于边界实现方式。

Abstract: We investigate the role of boundary conditions in the critical behaviour of the ferromagnetic Ising model on regular hyperbolic tessellations. Using worm and Metropolis simulations, we compare open boundaries with wired boundaries implemented through a common auxiliary spin. For open boundaries, the susceptibility exhibits volume-based finite-size scaling with non-mean-field scaling exponents that are approximately stable across four tessellations, supporting the possibility of a common universality class. Comparisons with Binder crossings and susceptibility peaks reveal substantial finite-size effects. For wired boundaries, the central-to-auxiliary spin correlator exhibits radial scaling consistent with a mean-field magnetization exponent. The central-spin response to a uniform magnetic field provides a further consistency test of $δ=3$. Layer-resolved responses to a weak boundary field also reveal an enhancement of the induced magnetization towards the interior. Our results highlight the importance of boundary conditions and the distinction between interior and whole-system observables in characterizing critical phenomena on non-amenable lattices.

</details>


### [113] [Exact and Near-Exact Inverse Renormalization Group with Constrained Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07641)
*Dimitrios Bachtis*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束马尔可夫过程的逆重整化群方法，实现了在不动点处的精确逆变换，并在不动点附近近似精确；通过在逆重整化构型上直接优化RG变换，提升了高维系统模拟的计算稳定性；应用于三维φ⁴量子场论，成功生成大体积（128³）近平衡构型并提取临界指数，且无临界慢化现象（z≈0.03）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重整化群（RG）变换是不可逆的，导致从粗格点恢复细格点物理信息困难；为克服临界慢化、提升大尺度蒙特卡洛模拟效率，需发展严格或近似精确的逆RG方法。

Method: 引入约束马尔可夫过程：在细格点区块内采样自由度，同时强制其满足前向RG变换对粗格点自由度的约束；理论证明该过程在RG不动点处实现精确逆变换，在其邻域内近似精确；进一步在逆RG构型上优化RG变换，使作用量自映射以保障数值稳定性。

Result: 在三维φ⁴模型（V=16³初始构型）上成功实现逆RG至V=128³，生成未关联、近平衡构型；提取两个临界指数；观测到极小动态临界指数z=0.03(2)，表明无临界慢化。

Conclusion: 约束马尔可夫过程为逆RG提供了严格基础，可在不动点及邻域实现高精度逆变换；该方法兼具理论严谨性与计算实用性，为高维强关联系统的大规模模拟提供了新范式。

Abstract: We establish conditions for exactness of the inverse renormalization group via constrained Markov processes, a class of Monte Carlo simulations which sample the degrees of freedom within a block on a fine lattice under the constraint that they remain consistent with the corresponding degree of freedom on the coarse lattice according to a forward renormalization group transformation. We show analytically, for an arbitrary transformation, that constrained Markov processes correspond to an exact inversion of the renormalization group at the fixed point action, and to a near-exact inversion in the vicinity of the fixed point. We then optimize renormalization group transformations directly on inversely renormalized configurations to effectively map the action onto itself and ensure computational stability in the generation of configurations for high-dimensional systems. Starting from a set of uncorrelated configurations for the three-dimensional $φ^{4}$ quantum field theory at lattice volume $V=16^{3}$, we invert the renormalization group with constrained Monte Carlo to construct a set of uncorrelated, near-equilibrium configurations for $V=128^{3}$ and extract two critical exponents of the system. The inversion exhibits no critical slowing down, with dynamic critical exponent $z=0.03(2)$.

</details>


### [114] [First Overlap Formulation and Perturbative Results for Supersymmetric QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07745)
*Marios Costa,Elias Antoniou,Herodotos Herodotou,Eleni Ioannou,Constantinos Kartoudes,Gregoris Spanoudes,Haralambos Panagopoulos*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重叠费米子的N=1超对称量子色动力学（SQCD）格点公式，满足Ginsparg-Wilson关系，并通过引入辅助费米场构建保持格点修正手征对称性的Yukawa项；在一阶微扰计算中确定了各类场和质量的抵消项，为后续耦合常数精细调节提供了坚实基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一种能保持手征对称性的N=1超对称QCD格点离散化方案，以克服Wilson型费米子所需大量抵消项的问题，并为超对称性在格点上的精确实现与微扰研究奠定基础。

Method: 采用满足Ginsparg-Wilson关系的重叠费米子描述夸克与胶微子；借鉴Lüscher策略，引入辅助Dirac（夸克）和Majorana（胶微子）场来构造手征对称的Yukawa项；对辅助场做泛函积分后得到局域相互作用项；结合Wilson规范作用量，开展包含夸克与标量夸克质量的一阶微扰计算。

Result: 成功导出夸克、胶子、胶微子和标量夸克的自能，并据此明确确定了各场与质量的格点抵消项；证实相比Wilson费米子，所需抵消项数目显著减少。

Conclusion: 该格点SQCD公式在保持手征对称性方面具有一致性与鲁棒性，为未来系统性地进行超对称耦合常数（规范、Yukawa、四次）的微扰精细调节提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: We present a lattice formulation of N=1 supersymmetric QCD (SQCD) based on overlap fermions, providing a discretization consistent with the Ginsparg-Wilson relation. Particular emphasis is placed on the construction of Yukawa terms that preserve a lattice-modified chiral symmetry involving overlap quarks and gluinos. To realize this modified chiral symmetry in the Yukawa sector, we follow Lüscher's strategy and introduce auxiliary Dirac and Majorana fermionic fields for the quark and gluino fields, respectively. After functional integration over these auxiliary fields, ultralocal interaction terms arise as additional contributions to the lattice action. Within this framework, and using the Wilson gauge action for gluons, we also carry out one-loop perturbative calculations. In particular, we compute the self-energies of the quark, gluon, gluino, and squark fields in the presence of quark and squark masses, enabling an unambiguous determination of the corresponding field and mass counterterms on the lattice. This work provides a robust foundation for future perturbative determinations of the fine-tunings of the gauge, Yukawa, and quartic couplings, and already shows, as expected, a reduction in the number of required counterterms relative to Wilson-type fermions.

</details>


### [115] [Quark-mass dependence of two-nucleon scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07864)
*Jorge Baeza-Ballesteros,André Baião-Raposo,Davide Laudicina,Malcolm Lazarow,Thomas R. Richardson,Miguel Salg,John Bulava,Jeremy R. Green,Andrew Hanlon,Renwick J. Hudspith,Daniel Mohler,Colin Morningstar,Amy Nicholson,Fernando Romero-López,André Walker-Loud,Hartmut Wittig*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD计算研究了在不同π介子质量下（约200、280和710 MeV）核子-核子散射的夸克质量依赖性，结合手征有效场论分析散射长度与有效力程随π质量的变化，并预测氘核在π质量约220 MeV附近解束缚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解可见宇宙性质如何依赖标准模型参数，需从第一性原理出发进行理论计算；本文旨在探究低能核子-核子散射（特别是氘核与双中子通道）对夸克质量（表现为π介子质量）的依赖关系。

Method: 采用格点QCD在多个π介子质量（≈200, 280, 710 MeV）下进行数值模拟，利用含单π交换长程效应的有限体积量子化条件提取散射振幅、相移和阈值参数；并基于Weinberg和Kaplan-Savage-Wise两种手征幂计数方案进行π质量依赖性拟合与外推。

Result: 获得了散射长度和有效力程随π质量变化的定量行为；估算出物理点处逆散射长度和结合动量对π质量的导数；两种手征幂计数均一致表明氘核在π质量≈220 MeV附近解束缚。

Conclusion: 氘核的束缚态对轻夸克质量高度敏感，其稳定性边界可由格点QCD与手征有效理论协同确定；该结果为连接粒子物理参数与核物理可观测量提供了关键定量依据。

Abstract: Understanding how the properties of the visible Universe depend on the parameters of the Standard Model of particle physics requires \emph{ab initio} theoretical computations. To that end, we investigate the quark-mass dependence of low-energy nucleon-nucleon scattering in the deuteron and dineutron channels. We perform lattice-QCD calculations at pion masses $M_π\approx 200$ and $280$ MeV and compare their results to phenomenology at the physical point and to a previous calculation at $M_π\approx 710$ MeV. Scattering amplitudes, phase shifts, and threshold parameters are determined from finite-volume spectra using quantization conditions that explicitly incorporate long-distance effects from one-pion exchange. We analyze the pion-mass dependence of scattering lengths and effective ranges using chiral effective field theory with Weinberg and Kaplan-Savage-Wise power countings. From this, we estimate the pion-mass derivatives of the inverse scattering lengths and binding momentum, at the physical point. Both power countings indicate that the deuteron unbinds near $M_π\approx 220$ MeV.

</details>


### [116] [Primitive Quantum Gates for an $SU(3)$ Discrete Subgroup: $Σ(216\times3)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08161)
*Erik J. Gustafson,Henry Lamm,Edison Murairi,Sebastian Osorio Perez*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文构建了用于数字量子模拟SU(3)离散子群Δ(648)的基本门集，包括五种算法原语，并估算了在特定粘滞系数计算任务下的容错量子资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现对SU(3)离散子群Δ(648)的高效数字量子模拟，需设计适配该群结构的专用量子门集，以支持如剪切粘滞系数等物理量的高精度计算。

Method: 提出并实现了五种算法原语：群逆元、群乘法、迹、群傅里叶变换和动能相位门，并分别给出了其在量子比特和量子比特-量子三态混合架构下的具体量子电路分解。

Result: 在10^3空间格点、50时间步、总合成误差预算10^{-8}的剪切粘滞系数基准计算中，估算出量子比特架构需2.3–6.8×10^{10}个T门，量子比特-量子三态架构需1.3–5.9×10^{12}个R门。

Conclusion: 所构造的Δ(648)专用门集可有效支撑相关强关联系统的量子模拟，且不同硬件架构下资源开销差异显著，为后续容错量子模拟的硬件选择与优化提供了量化依据。

Abstract: We construct a primitive gate set for the digital quantum simulation of the $648$-element discrete subgroup of $SU(3)$, $\dsb$. The five algorithmic primitives are the inversion, group multiplication, trace, group Fourier transform, and kinetic-phase gates, for which we provide qubit and qutrit--qubit decompositions. For a fiducial shear-viscosity calculation on a $10^3$ spatial lattice with $50$ time steps and a total synthesis-error budget of $10^{-8}$, the estimated fault-tolerant costs are $2.3$--$6.8\times10^{10}$ $T$ gates in the qubit architecture and $1.3$--$5.9\times10^{12}$ $R$ gates in the qutrit--qubit architecture.

</details>
