### [117] [Matrix Theory from Holography II: Beyond Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06999)
*Shota Komatsu,Eunwoo Lee,Chintan Patel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了三维N=4 ADHM理论大电荷扇区、BMN矩阵模型与光锥紧致化M理论在最大超对称pp波背景下的新三重对偶；通过超对称局域化，证明了拓扑子扇区中相关函数（含BMN耦合依赖）的一致性，并揭示了ADHM中的磁单极泡涨落与BMN模型中瞬子的深层联系；还给出了IIB弦论对应版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展先前在AdS/CFT框架下实现的M理论矩阵理论，探索更广泛的对偶结构，特别是超越态计数、进入相关函数层面的精确匹配，并建立单极物理与瞬子物理之间的联系。

Method: 采用三重标度极限分析超共形指数；运用超对称局域化技术计算并比对ADHM理论与BMN模型在各磁通扇区中的相关函数、测度及算符插入；结合镜像对称，将单极算符映射到镜像项链理论的Higgs分支绕数算符；构造IIB对应体系并检验超共形指数匹配。

Result: 1) ADHM理论超共形指数在三重标度极限下等于BMN模型的大正则指数，磁通扇区对应模糊球真空；2) 各磁通扇区中拓扑子扇区的相关函数、局域化测度和算符插入完全匹配；3) 单极泡涨落机制精确复现BMN瞬子的经典作用量权重；4) IIB对应中Z_K轨道理论超共形指数重现质量变形ABJM理论真空简并度。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一个精确的三重对偶框架，不仅统一了不同理论描述，而且将对偶性从态计数提升至动力学相关函数层面；揭示了单极物理与瞬子物理的内在统一性，并为M理论与弦论在pp波背景下的非微扰结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Extending our earlier realization of Matrix Theory for M-theory within AdS/CFT, we propose and test a new triality among a large-charge sector of three-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ ADHM theory, the BMN matrix model, and lightlike-compactified M-theory on the maximally supersymmetric pp-wave. In an appropriate triple-scaling limit, the superconformal index of ADHM theory becomes the grand-canonical index of the BMN model, with magnetic-flux sectors corresponding to fuzzy-sphere vacua. But the real interest of this new setup is that we can go beyond counting states. Using supersymmetric localization, we show that correlation functions in topological subsectors agree as well, including their nontrivial dependence on the BMN coupling. In each magnetic-flux sector, both the localization measure and the operator insertions can be matched precisely with those of the corresponding BMN vacuum. Our results also point to a deeper connection between monopole bubbling in ADHM theory and instantons in the BMN model. For minuscule monopole charges, ADHM correlators map directly to topological correlators in the trivial BMN vacuum. For non-minuscule charges, monopole bubbling produces additional screened sectors, whose relative weights reproduce the classical actions of BMN instantons. Mirror symmetry offers another perspective on this structure, mapping the monopole operators to Higgs-branch ``winding" operators in the mirror necklace theory. Finally, we discuss a type IIB counterpart, connecting a large-charge sector of four-dimensional $\mathbb Z_K$-orbifold quiver theories, the DLCQ of type IIB string theory on the pp-wave, and level-one mass-deformed ABJM theory on $\mathbb R_t\times T^2$. As a first test, we show that the superconformal index of the orbifold theory reproduces the vacuum degeneracy of the mass-deformed ABJM theory.

</details>


### [118] [Multipartite Entanglement, TQFT Partition Functions and PL Bordism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07000)
*Michele Del Zotto,Abhijit Gadde,Pavel Putrov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了gapped系统基态的多不变量与低能拓扑量子场论（TQFT）配分函数之间的关系，指出当流形M为可定向零 bordant 时，其配分函数Z(M)可由一组显式、与态无关的多不变量确定（偶维需乘以Z(S^d)^{χ(M)/2}，奇维可消去）；而障碍由有向bordism群决定，在d=4时具体表现为流形的符号差σ(M)，且挠不变量不受阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解gapped系统基态多不变量如何编码低能TQFT的拓扑信息，并分离出其中受局部三角剖分影响的非普适因子。

Method: 基于有向bordism理论分析多不变量与TQFT配分函数的关系，结合示性类（如第一Pontryagin类p₁、Stiefel-Whitney类w₂w₃）和具体模型（如4+1维可逆相）进行计算与验证。

Result: 明确了Z(M)可被提取的充要条件是M为null-bordant；在d=4时障碍即为符号差σ(M)；并精确计算了含挠不变量（如(-1)^{∫w₂w₃}）的实例，揭示Dold流形与CP²×S¹对应多不变量符号相反。

Conclusion: 多不变量能有效提取TQFT配分函数当且仅当流形满足特定bordism条件；非普适因子的消除依赖于拓扑障碍的代数结构，特别是bordism群的挠子群与示性类的局域可表性。

Abstract: In a previous work we conjectured that the multi-invariants of the ground state of a gapped $(d-1)+1$-dimensional system, labeled by bipartite $(d+1)$-edge-colored graphs encoding closed oriented $d$-manifolds $M$, compute the partition functions $Z(M)$ of the low energy topological quantum field theory (TQFT), up to non-universal factors determined by the local structure of the triangulation. In this paper we determine which combinations of partition functions can be isolated from the non-universal factors. The answer is governed by oriented bordism. If $M$ is null-bordant, $Z(M)$ is obtained from an explicit, state-independent finite family of multi-invariants, up to a power of $Z(S^d)$ which is fixed to be $Z(S^d)^{χ(M)/2}$ for even $d$ and can be removed for odd $d$. Conversely, the obstruction is measured by a quotient of the group of "residues" of colored graphs which receives a surjective map from the oriented bordism group, with kernel precisely the torsion subgroup as far as smooth manifolds are concerned. In $d=4$ the obstruction is the signature: $Z(M)/Z(S^4)^{χ(M)/2}$ can be extracted if and only if $σ(M)=0$. This is due to the fact that the first Pontryagin class $p_1$ has a local expression and thus $e^{iθσ(M)}=e^{\frac{i}{3}θ\int_M p_1}$ cannot be separated from the non-universal factors. Torsion invariants, in contrast, are not obstructed: for the ground state of the $4+1$-dimensional invertible phase with partition function $(-1)^{\int w_2w_3}$ we compute the multi-invariants exactly, and two graphs with identical local data, encoding the Dold manifold and $\mathbb{CP}^2\times S^1$, give opposite signs.

</details>


### [119] [Fermion$\text{-}$Monopole Scattering: A Unified Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07001)
*Hao Geng,Zekai Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了无质量手性费米子在最小荷BPS 't Hooft-Polyakov磁单极子散射过程，通过构建低能费米子-转子统一描述，澄清了不同既有方案间的联系，解决了关于末态、能量守恒与反常散射振幅的表面矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对磁单极子散射费米子的不同理论方案强调不同方面，彼此联系不清，导致在末态、能量守恒和反常散射振幅等方面出现表面冲突，亟需统一理解。

Method: 基于磁单极子背景推导转子耦合，全程保留显式费米子算符；引入解耦辅助标量构造可精确量子化的对偶理论；分析转子顶点谱性质及红外交换关系，并直接从紧致转子绕数构型激发的费米子零模计算反常关联函数。

Result: 发现转子顶点具有直至零能的连续谱支撑，其红外交换关系表明它即为出射费米子的扭结打扮（twist dressing）；反常费米子关联函数可直接由转子绕数激发的零模导出。

Conclusion: 转子动力学、扭结出射态与零模起源的反常关联函数三者被统一起来，消除了先前方案间的表面矛盾，表明它们在共同适用范围内构成自洽整体。

Abstract: We revisit the scattering of massless chiral fermions by a minimally charged BPS 't Hooft-Polyakov monopole. Existing proposals emphasize different aspects of this process and their connections are often unclear, giving rise to apparent conflicts concerning the outgoing states, energy conservation, and anomalous scattering amplitudes. We clarify the connection between different proposals within a common low-energy fermion-rotor description. Starting from the monopole background, we derive the rotor coupling and retain explicit fermion operators throughout the analysis. By adjoining a decoupled auxiliary scalar, we construct a dual theory that permits exact quantization. We show that the rotor vertex has a continuous spectral support down to zero energy and that its infrared exchange relations identify it as the twist dressing of the outgoing fermion. We also compute anomalous fermion correlators directly from fermion zero modes generated by winding configurations of the compact rotor. These results connect rotor dynamics, twisted outgoing states, and the zero-mode origin of anomalous correlators, resolving apparent conflicts and showing how the existing proposals fit into a mutually consistent picture within their common regime of applicability.

</details>


### [120] [Asymptotic safety is not generic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07002)
*Francesco Del Porro,Francesco Ferrarin,Alessia Platania*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过研究SU(2)和SU(3)手征微扰理论（具有与引力相似的不可重整性和非线性相互作用），利用泛函重整化群方法在四阶导数截断下未发现非平凡紫外不动点，表明渐近安全并非普遍现象，而是特定系统的特有性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究渐近安全是否是引力等理论中普遍存在的性质，还是仅限于某些特殊系统；尤其关注非平凡beta函数零点（即紫外不动点）出现的普遍性。

Method: 采用泛函重整化群（FRG）方法，在四阶导数截断下系统研究SU(2)和SU(3)手征微扰理论的动力学行为。

Result: 在所研究的手征模型中未发现相互作用的紫外不动点，与引力中观测到的Reuter不动点形成对比。

Conclusion: 渐近安全并非一般场论的普适特性，而更可能是某些特定系统（如量子引力）所独有的特征。

Abstract: Asymptotically safe quantum gravity proposes a non-perturbative ultraviolet completion of gravity in which the high-energy behavior is controlled by an ultraviolet fixed point---the Reuter fixed point---for which there is robust evidence from a wide range of settings and truncations. The existence of asymptotic safety requires non-trivial zeros of beta functions, raising the question of how generic such fixed-point solutions are. In this work, we tackle this issue by investigating chiral perturbation theories. We focus on SU(2) and SU(3) chiral models, which share key features with gravity such as perturbative non-renormalizability or nonlinear interactions, but which are known to admit a different ultraviolet completion via new degrees of freedom. Using the functional renormalization group, we study these theories within a quartic-derivative truncation and find no evidence for an interacting fixed point. This result suggests that asymptotic safety is not generic, but rather a feature pertaining to certain special systems.

</details>


### [121] [Conformal Bootstrap Unblocked](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07004)
*Li-Yuan Chiang,David Poland*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖算符乘积展开（OPE）和共形块的新型共形自举方法，直接基于共形对称性、反射正定性和交叉对称性，通过构造局域算符生成态的Gram矩阵并施加共形生成元的谱约束，构建半定规划层级来直接约束关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形自举严重依赖OPE和共形块，限制了其在低维或非标准情形下的适用性；本文旨在建立一种更基础、更普适的自举框架。

Method: 基于共形对称性、反射正定性和交叉对称性构建Gram矩阵，利用共形生成元施加谱约束，形成半定规划（SDP）层级，直接约束n点关联函数。

Result: 在1–3维中成功实现该方法，获得关联函数与谱的严格界限，复现广义自由场行为及2D/3D Ising模型的典型‘kink’特征，并揭示不同构型下态的联合正定性可自然约束非自对偶运动学区域的关联函数。

Conclusion: 该方法为共形场论提供了一种更本源、更灵活的自举范式，拓展了数值共形自举的理论基础与适用范围。

Abstract: We develop a novel conformal bootstrap formulated directly in terms of conformal symmetry, reflection positivity, and crossing symmetry, without explicitly relying on the operator product expansion or conformal blocks. By constructing Gram matrices from states created by pairs of local operators and imposing spectral constraints through conformal generators, we obtain a hierarchy of semidefinite programs that directly constrain correlation functions. We demonstrate the method in one to three dimensions, obtaining correlator and spectral bounds that capture generalized free field behavior and reproduce the characteristic Ising kinks in two and three dimensions. We further show that joint positivity of states at different insertion configurations naturally constrains correlators away from self-dual kinematics.

</details>


### [122] [On Entanglement Dynamics in Holographic Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07010)
*Hanzhi Jiang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用AdS/CFT对偶研究强耦合量子场论中纠缠熵的实时动力学，涵盖SSD变形CFT、混沌系统中的纠缠膜描述、反射熵的膜理论推广及多体纠缠信号的时间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠熵在相互作用且具有非平凡时间依赖性的理论中计算困难，亟需新方法理解其动力学行为。

Method: 基于AdS/CFT对偶，结合二维共形场论（CFT）解析解、纠缠膜理论、全息计算与相关变形（如SSD、相关算符变形）进行分析。

Result: 验证了SSD驱动下CFT与全息EE演化的对应；揭示2d CFT中退化膜张力特性；指出反射熵需额外自由度；证明相关变形可诱导非退化张力及BTZ黑洞内Kasner结构；构建高维反射熵膜理论并应用于多体纠缠演化分析。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵与反射熵的动力学可通过几何与有效膜理论统一描述，其结构深刻反映时空几何、混沌性质与量子信息传播之间的内在联系。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy (EE) is central to modern theoretical physics, yet its calculation in interacting theories with nontrivial time dependence remains challenging. In this thesis, we investigate the real-time dynamics of EE in strongly coupled quantum field theories (QFTs) using the Anti-de Sitter/Conformal Field Theory (AdS/CFT) correspondence. We first examine EE evolution in an exactly solvable two-dimensional conformal field theory (CFT) with an inhomogeneous sine-square deformation (SSD). We argue that the unconventional time evolution driven by the SSD Hamiltonian can be viewed as a change in spacetime foliation, producing new horizons in the dual three-dimensional bulk. Holographic EE calculations agree with CFT results in all comparable cases. We then study entanglement dynamics in generic chaotic quantum many-body systems. In the limit of late time and large regions, these dynamics are believed to admit an effective entanglement membrane description. In 2d CFTs, a degenerate membrane tension reproduces several entropy dynamics results, unlike the non-degenerate tension in higher dimensions. Using holography, we show that capturing reflected entropy in 2d CFT requires an additional degree of freedom in the membrane description. We further show that a conventional non-degenerate tension emerges when the CFT undergoes a relevant deformation, holographically dual to a planar Banados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black hole with scalar hair and an interior Kasner universe. We then develop a membrane effective theory for reflected entropy dynamics in higher dimensions. Finally, we apply the membrane theories for reflected entropy to study the time evolution of multipartite entanglement signals. We found that scrambling dynamics generate multipartite entanglement with rich structures.

</details>


### [123] [Constraining the spectral gap of 2d CFTs just above c=1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07011)
*Nathan Benjamin,Kevin Langhoff,Wei Li,Jesse Thaler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用模引导（modular bootstrap）数值研究了中心荷c>1的二维紧致幺正共形场论（2d CFTs）的空间，重点在于约束其能谱间隙Δ_gap（即非真空原初算符的最小标度维数），并引入原初算符简并度为正整数这一关键整性条件，显著收紧了c接近1时的间隙上界，暗示当c→1⁺时Δ_gap可能趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解紧致幺正2d CFT在c>1时的普适结构限制，特别是能谱间隙的可能取值范围，而传统模引导未充分考虑物理谱的整数简并度约束。

Method: 采用模引导方法，结合原始（primal）谱搜索与对偶（dual）严格排除两种数值策略，并显式施加原初算符简并度为正整数的物理约束。

Result: 在c非常接近1（如c−1=3×10⁻³）时，整性约束使严格上界从Δ_gap≲0.5大幅收紧至Δ_gap<0.041；数值结果支持当c→1⁺时谱间隙趋近于零的猜想。

Conclusion: 原初算符简并度的整性是一个强约束，在小c−1区域显著压缩允许的谱间隙空间，暗示c=1可能是紧致幺正2d CFT谱间隙消失的临界点。

Abstract: We numerically study the space of compact unitary two-dimensional conformal field theories (2d CFTs) with central charge $c>1$ using the modular bootstrap. We focus on bounding the spectral gap $Δ_{\rm gap}$ of such theories, namely the smallest scaling dimension among the non-vacuum primary operators. We use both a primal method that directly searches for consistent spectra and a dual method that yields rigorous exclusions. A key feature of our analysis is requiring the degeneracies of primary operators to be positive integers. At $c$ very near $1$, the integrality constraint greatly reduces the allowed spectral gap in both methods. For example, at $c-1 = 3\times 10^{-3}$ integrality strengthens the rigorous bound from $Δ_{\rm gap} \lesssim 0.5$ to $Δ_{\rm gap} < 0.041$. Our results suggest that the spectral gap might vanish as $c$ approaches $1$ from above.

</details>


### [124] [Chern-Simons Interfaces and Minimal Models from Quantum Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07012)
*Suriyah Rajalingam Kannagi,Sergio Sanjurjo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过q-形变费米子链来描述二维狄拉克费米子与SU(N)_k陈-西蒙斯理论耦合所形成的非手性界面的连续极限，并利用量子群给出算符代数的代数视角；同时推导出涉及重子数与晶格平移的Lieb-Schultz-Mattis约束，并在单位填充下得到标准任意子链哈密顿量，为红外中N个被晶格平移置换的最小模型扇区的存在性提供了反常论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维狄拉克费米子与拓扑规范场耦合所生成的非手性界面的低能有效描述及其对称性保护性质。

Method: 将一维复费米子链与连续SU(N)_k陈-西蒙斯场耦合实现q-形变，借助量子群刻画算符代数，并结合反常匹配与Lieb-Schultz-Mattis型论证分析对称性限制。

Result: 建立了q-形变费米子链作为非手性界面连续极限的新描述；导出了重子数-平移对称性的LSM约束；在单位填充下恢复标准任意子链，并论证红外中出现N个被平移置换的W_N最小模型扇区。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了任意子链的范畴论描述与量子群代数结构，并通过反常匹配为低能涌现对称性破缺模式提供了严格约束。

Abstract: Coupling two-dimensional fundamental Dirac fermions to $SU(N)_k$ Chern-Simons theory produces non-chiral interfaces that in the infrared flow to a compact boson and a $W_N$ minimal model. We propose a description of these interfaces as continuum limits of $q$-deformed fermion chains. The $q$-deformation is achieved by coupling a chain of one-dimensional fundamental complex fermions to $SU(N)_k$ Chern-Simons theory, keeping the underlying topological gauge field continuous. The operator algebra is controlled by quantum groups, which provide an algebraic perspective on the usual categorical formulation of anyon chains. As an application, we derive a Lieb-Schultz-Mattis constraint involving baryon-number and lattice translation, obstructing a unique symmetric gapped ground state. At unit filling, we gap out the compact boson sector with a Mott term and obtain standard anyon chain Hamiltonians previously studied in the literature. This provides an anomaly argument for the existence of $N$ copies of minimal model sectors permuted by lattice translation in the infrared.

</details>


### [125] [Constraint Variance Identity and the Matter-Geometry Fluctuation Relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07015)
*Yuki Yokokura*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导出物质-几何涨落关系，建立了时空钟涨落与物质涨落之间的动力学联系，并给出物质能量与关系时间之间的不确定性关系，表明时空的稳定存在依赖于物质的量子涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子引力框架下时空与物质之间的基本关系，特别是物理态下量子哈密顿约束为零所蕴含的深层含义。

Method: 在minisuperspace中对可分离哈密顿量应用量子哈密顿约束及其方差为零的条件，并结合Born-Oppenheimer近似进行推导。

Result: 得到物质-几何涨落关系、时空钟抗量子噪声的定量稳定性条件，以及物质能量与关系时间的不确定性关系。

Conclusion: 时空的稳定存在以物质的量子涨落为必要条件。

Abstract: The quantum Hamiltonian constraint vanishes for physical states. For separable Hamiltonians in minisuperspace, its vanishing variance yields a fundamental identity. Under the Born-Oppenheimer approximation, we derive the Matter-Geometry Fluctuation Relation, dynamically linking spacetime clock fluctuations to matter fluctuations. This establishes a quantitative stability condition for the clock against total quantum noise. Finally, we obtain an uncertainty relation between matter energy and relational time. The stable existence of spacetime thus demands quantum fluctuations in matter.

</details>


### [126] [Running Couplings in CP Violating Abelian Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07016)
*Joshua Newey,John Terning,Christopher B. Verhaaren*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了无质量光子与磁单极子（dyons）耦合的一圈和两圈跑动耦合常数，包含CP破坏项；发现电、磁耦合常数反向跑动但其行列式det C保持不变，从而保证狄拉克-施温格-兹万策格电荷量子化条件及成对螺旋度恒定；并讨论了θ角跑动与物理CP破坏的关系，以及CP-奇耦合在源项与光子动能项间可通过场重定义等价转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带CP破坏项的光子-磁单极子系统中电、磁耦合的量子化与跑动行为，特别是电荷量子化条件如何在量子修正下保持，以及CP破坏参数θ的物理意义与重整化行为。

Method: 基于量子场论，计算一阶和二阶微扰（一圈和两圈）下的β函数；分析耦合矩阵C及其转置乘积C^T C与行列式det C的重整化群流；研究CP-奇项在不同作用量项（源项 vs 动能项）间的等价性；并与Argyres-Douglas β函数猜想进行比对。

Result: 1) 电、磁耦合通过耦合矩阵C反向跑动，但det C在跑动中严格守恒；2) Coulomb型相互作用连续跑动，而Dirac-Schwinger-Zwanziger电荷量子化条件及成对螺旋度保持固定；3) CP-奇耦合θ的跑动依赖于谱、质量相位与阈值修正；4) θ可经可逆场重定义在源项与光子动能项之间转移，二者等价；5) 结果支持Argyres-Douglas β函数猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了磁单极子理论中电-磁对偶性与量子反常之间的深层联系：det C守恒体现了拓扑保护的量子化条件，而θ的可重定义性表明其物理效应需结合完整谱结构理解；为强耦合S对偶与CP破坏的统一描述提供了微观基础。

Abstract: We calculate the one- and two-loop running couplings for a massless photon coupled to dyons, including CP violating couplings. The electric and magnetic source vertices in the most general theory form a real coupling matrix $C$, while a current-current interaction depends on $C^T C$ and the quantized determinant $\det C$. These determine, respectively, the continuously running Coulombic interactions and the fixed antisymmetric Dirac--Schwinger--Zwanziger charge quantization condition which also determines pairwise helicity. The running preserves $\det C$, showing that the electric and magnetic couplings run inversely while every pairwise helicity remains constant. We comment on how the running of the CP-odd coupling $θ$ relates to physical CP violation, which also depends on the spectrum, mass phases, and threshold corrections. We also show that an invertible field redefinition moves the CP-odd coupling between the current source terms and the photon kinetic terms; these are two descriptions of the same local interaction. Finally, we show that the result agrees with the Argyres--Douglas $β$ function ansatz.

</details>


### [127] [Charged AdS$_{3}$ black branes as marginally deformed two-dimensional CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07019)
*Suting Zhao,Gabriel Arenas-Henriquez,Yizhou Lu,Hamed Adami,René Meyer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了边界关联函数如何编码AdS₃黑洞膜的重整化欧几里得作用量对电荷的依赖性，发现其电荷展开系数由卡塔兰数决定，并通过关联函数计算了二次和四次项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS/CFT中边界关联函数与体理论热力学量（如作用量）之间的精确对应关系，特别是电荷如何影响重整化作用量。

Method: 基于三维Einstein-Maxwell理论，利用AdS₃黑膜背景下的边界源-算符对应、连通关联函数（2点和4点）、Witten图及红外截断积分方法，计算作用量对电荷密度的展开系数。

Result: 证明在固定温度下，经典作用量关于电荷密度是偶函数；二次项由标量两点函数和源的平移对称性决定，给出物质Weyl反常；四次项由树图引力子交换贡献，经源积分后复现卡塔兰系数；更高阶项由对应阶连通Witten图积分给出。

Conclusion: 边界连通关联函数系统地编码了体理论热力学作用量的电荷依赖结构，其展开系数由卡塔兰数普适控制，揭示了AdS₃中电荷、对称性与共形反常间的深层联系。

Abstract: We study how boundary correlation functions encode the charge dependence of the renormalised Euclidean action of an AdS$_3$ black brane. In three-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell theory, the Maxwell field has logarithmic asymptotics and is locally dual to a massless pseudoscalar. In the canonical ensemble, the charge density fixes the slope of a spatially linear source for the dual marginal operator. At fixed temperature, the classical action is even in the charge density due to reflection symmetry $Φ\to-Φ$, and its charge expansion coefficients are governed by Catalan numbers. We compute the quadratic and quartic terms from connected correlation functions around the neutral brane. The scalar two-point function and the shift symmetry of its source determine the matter Weyl anomaly, which fixes the logarithmic temperature dependence of the quadratic term in the charge expansion. At quartic order, the connected four-point function comes from tree-level graviton exchange. Integrating it against the linear source, with an infrared cutoff on the overall scale of the integral, reproduces the quartic coefficient of the action per unit length. At each higher order, the corresponding Catalan coefficient predicts the integrated sum of all connected tree-level Witten diagrams.

</details>


### [128] [Finite is not enough: Triviality bounds in nonlocal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07026)
*Fabian Wagner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出，紫外有限理论不一定紫外完备，因此仅靠消除紫外发散不足以支持量子引力中时空离散化的观点；通过研究含紫外压制因子的四维φ⁴理论的一圈动量减除流，发现非局域尺度以上跑动被冻结，但仍可能在压制起效前遭遇朗道极点；为保证全尺度的微扰可控性，导出了红外耦合的严格界限，该界限在所考察例子中变化仅约百分之一；类似界限预期也适用于离散时空模型，表明要求紫外完备性可提升非局域理论的可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑仅以消除紫外发散为理由支持量子引力中时空离散化的观点，强调紫外有限性不等价于紫外完备性。

Method: 推导四维φ⁴理论中含紫外压制形式因子的一圈动量减除重整化群流，分析非局域尺度对耦合常数跑动的影响，并施加全尺度微扰可控性条件以约束红外耦合。

Result: 发现即使存在紫外压制，理论仍可能在压制起效前遭遇朗道极点；导出红外耦合的普适性上限，其在不同非局域模型间变化仅约1%；表明紫外完备性要求可显著提升非局域理论的可预测性。

Conclusion: 仅靠紫外有限性不足以论证时空离散；紫外完备性是更严格的物理要求，能增强非局域量子引力模型（如离散时空）的理论约束力与预测能力。

Abstract: An ultraviolet-finite theory need not be ultraviolet complete. This observation weakens the case for spacetime discreteness in quantum gravity if that case rests on removing ultraviolet divergences alone. We arrive at this observation by deriving the one-loop momentum-subtraction flow in four-dimensional $φ^4$ theories with ultraviolet-suppressing form factors. The suppression freezes the running above the nonlocality scale, but the theory can hit a Landau pole before suppression sets in. We obtain a bound on the infrared coupling by requiring perturbative control at all scales. This bound varies only at the percent level across the examples we consider. We expect similar bounds in models of discrete spacetime. Thus, requiring ultraviolet completeness improves the predictivity of nonlocal theories.

</details>


### [129] [Minimal massive completions of partially broken higher-spin models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07057)
*E. L. Mendonça,M. I. C. Maués*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分破缺Fronsdal（PBF）模型及其质量化补全问题，发现其在引入质量项和辅助场后，低自旋模式被消除，且对s=3和s=4情形构造出唯一（至多场重定义）的最小辅助场结构；结果表明PBF模型的质量化版本等价于ADY模型，而Skvortsov–Vasiliev（SV）模型的质量化版本则可保留类Weyl对称性或使参数c在质量极限下存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究部分破缺Fronsdal（PBF）模型是否存在真正新颖的质量化补全，特别是检验其与已知高自旋质量模型（如ADY、SV）的关系及参数c在质量极限下的命运。

Method: 在PBF模型中添加质量项和最多含二阶导数的一般局域辅助场，通过要求所有低自旋自由度不具动力学性质，系统求解约束条件；对s=3和s=4情形显式构造并分析辅助场结构，并推广至任意整数自旋；对SV模型采用类似策略，在一般和受限ansatz下分别构造其质量化版本。

Result: 对PBF模型：s=3需两个标量辅助场（一为代数消去型，一为ADY标量）；s=4需一个代数量子矢量；该机制可推广至任意s，且所得最小辅助内容下参数c在质量极限中消失，h的相关函数与ADY模型一致。对SV模型：存在保留Weyl类对称性的质量化版本，其辅助场由无低自旋模条件唯一确定；对部分破缺SV模型，无需额外辅助场，且c通过与无质量理论相同的接触项在质量极限中存活。

Conclusion: PBF模型不存在真正独立于ADY模型的质量化补全，其最小辅助结构自然导向ADY模型；而SV模型的质量化更具灵活性，既可保留对称性，也可使原始参数c在质量极限中非平凡地存活，揭示了不同高自旋质量化路径的本质差异。

Abstract: The partially broken Fronsdal (PBF) model describes massless particles of arbitrary integer spin $s$ with a gauge symmetry smaller than Fronsdal's, and differs from it only by the coefficient $c$ of the term $(\p\cdot h')^2$. We ask whether it admits a genuinely new massive completion. Adding a mass term and the most general local auxiliary sector with at most two derivatives, and demanding that all lower-spin sectors be non-dynamical, we show that for $s=3$ a single auxiliary scalar is never enough, while two scalars give a solution unique up to field redefinitions: one scalar is algebraic and its elimination exactly undoes the deformation, while the other is the auxiliary scalar of the Aragone--Deser--Yang (ADY) model. The same mechanism, with an algebraic vector, appears for $s=4$ and extends to arbitrary spin, and we conjecture that the resulting auxiliary content is minimal. In this minimal completion $c$ does not survive the mass: the correlation functions of $h$ coincide with those of the ADY model. For the Skvortsov--Vasiliev (SV) model, which belongs to the same family, we construct a massive version in which the Weyl-like symmetry survives the mass, and show that its auxiliary sector is fixed by the absence of lower-spin modes, within a general ansatz for $s=3$ and within a restricted one for $s=4$. For the partially broken SV model we obtain instead a massive completion which requires no auxiliary field beyond those of the massive SV model, and in which $c$ survives the mass through the same contact term as in the massless theory.

</details>


### [130] [On a Dual Heterotic Kodaira-Spencer Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07178)
*Siddhartha Mangamuri,Eirik Eik Svanes,Anuthi Tiwary*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有背景通量的卡拉比-丘三维流形上的对偶杂化Kodaira-Spencer理论，将Costello-Li提出的BCOV型Green-Schwarz机制引入经典形变复形，构造了一个由非全纯算子ar D控制的理论，并建立了其上同调与拟全纯扩展层上Cech上同调之间的Dolbeault型定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解带背景规范场通量的卡拉比-丘三维流形上的量子化弦理论变形结构，特别是处理规范反常并建立与BCOV理论的对偶联系。

Method: 引入Beltrami微分作为Green-Schwarz场；在对角\bar\partial^\dagger规范下构造含辅助(0,2)-形式的三次相互作用理论；利用L_3代数描述规范对称性；定义非全纯算子\bar D并分析其层论性质；证明Dolbeault型上同调定理。

Result: 实现了Costello-Li Green-Schwarz机制在杂化Kodaira-Spencer框架下的具体实现；一阶圈图规范反常被抵消；运动方程等价于Maurer-Cartan方程加Beltrami微分的散度自由约束；构造出拟全纯扩展层\widetilde{\mathscr{Q}}并证明H^{0,q}_{\bar D}(Q)\cong\check H^q(\widetilde{\mathscr{Q}})。

Conclusion: 该工作为带通量的卡拉比-丘几何上的量子形变理论提供了新的代数几何框架，将物理中的反常消除机制与层上同调深刻联系起来，为进一步纳入引力反常和完整量子化奠定基础。

Abstract: We consider a dual version of heterotic Kodaira-Spencer theory on a Calabi-Yau three-fold $X$ with background fluxes, related to the type I BCOV theory of Costello-Li. We implement the Green-Schwarz mechanism of Costello-Li, where the Beltrami differential plays the role of the Green-Schwarz field. On a compact Calabi-Yau three-fold with background gauge fluxes $F$, where $\mathrm{tr}(F^2)$ is trivial in cohomology, retaining gauge anomalies only and leaving gravitational anomalies to future work, we incorporate the Costello-Li Green-Schwarz mechanism into the classical deformation complex. This gives a theory governed by a nilpotent operator $\bar D$ on $Q=T^{1,0}X\oplus\operatorname{End}(V)\oplus T^{*1,0}X$, whose extension maps carry holomorphic derivatives, so that $(Q,\bar D)$ is not a holomorphic bundle. Using diagonal $\bar\partial^\dagger$-gauge, the one-loop gauge anomaly cancels, assuming that the extension maps of $\bar D$ do not modify it, which we argue is plausible. Introducing an auxiliary $(0,2)$-form, we construct a cubic interacting theory whose gauge symmetries are governed by an $L_3$ algebra. On compact Calabi-Yau three-folds, and for sufficiently small fields, we show that the equation of motion is equivalent to the Maurer-Cartan equation, together with a divergence-free constraint on the Beltrami differential. Finally we trivialise $\bar D$ locally to the Dolbeault operator. The (non-tensorial) transition maps glue into a quasi-holomorphic extension sheaf $\widetilde{\mathscr{Q}}$, for which we prove a Dolbeault theorem, $H^{0,q}_{\bar D}(Q)\cong\check H^q(\widetilde{\mathscr{Q}})$.

</details>


### [131] [In light of the Henneaux-Teitelboim action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07180)
*Andreas Gustavsson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个类似于Henneaux-Teitelboim作用量的六维洛伦兹流形上的作用量，其中时间方向为类光，并由一个共形类光Killing矢量U生成；证明该作用量存在需存在另一与U对易的共形类光Killing矢量V，且V可由两个共形Killing旋量的Dirac流实现；最后将该作用量推广至(1,0)超对称张量多重态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造适用于具类光时间方向的六维洛伦兹流形的物理作用量，并探索其与共形Killing矢量及旋量结构的内在联系。

Method: 基于共形类光Killing矢量U构造类似Henneaux-Teitelboim的作用量；分析其存在条件，引入对易的共形类光Killing矢量V；利用共形Killing旋量的Dirac流实现V；进一步将作用量延拓至(1,0)超对称张量多重态。

Result: 证明了此类作用量存在的充要条件是存在满足特定代数与几何性质的第二共形类光Killing矢量V；并成功将其推广到(1,0)超对称情形。

Conclusion: 六维类光时间背景下的Henneaux-Teitelboim型作用量与共形Killing旋量结构紧密关联，其超对称延拓是可行的，揭示了共形对称性、旋量几何与超对称之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We construct an action similar to the Henneaux-Teitelboim action on a six dimensional Lorentzian manifold where the time direction is chosen to be lightlike and generated by a conformal lightlike Killing vector $U$. We show that such an action exists only if there is another commuting light-like conformal Killing vector $V$ with properties that can be realized by the Dirac currents of two conformal Killing spinors. We extend the action to the $(1,0)$ supersymmetric tensor multiplet.

</details>


### [132] [Instantons from geometry in M2-brane sphere partition functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07186)
*Kiril Hristov,Ali Mert Yetkin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了M2-膜横向几何是否能捕捉其三维球面(S³)配分函数中指数小的效应，通过推广等变拓扑弦理论到四维卡拉比-丘流形(CY₄)，并研究其配分函数与膜瞬子效应的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究M2-膜横向几何能否描述其S³配分函数中指数小的物理效应，特别是膜瞬子贡献。

Method: 扩展等变拓扑弦理论至CY₄，研究CY₄上的带电线性σ模型的盘配分函数，经Gamma函数归一化和Mellin变换后，与WKB大势及谱迹进行比对。

Result: 成功复现多种理论（如ABJM、ADHM及含味M2-膜理论）的完整主导WKB大势与膜瞬子级数，包括无紧致曲线的ℂ⁴情形；对含紧致曲线的解析CY₄锥，需引入量子修正的经典介子约束；并给出尚未验证的膜瞬子几何预言。

Conclusion: CY₄横向几何确实可编码M2-膜S³配分函数中的指数小效应；盘配分函数经适当变换后精确等于经典谱迹，为M2-膜量子几何提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Can the geometry transverse to M2-branes capture effects that are exponentially small in their three-sphere ($S^3$) partition function? We investigate this question by extending beyond constant maps the proposal that M2-brane observables are encoded by equivariant topological strings on the transverse Calabi-Yau fourfold (CY$_4$). Our central object is the disk partition function of the CY$_4$ gauged linear sigma model, a quantum generalization of the equivariant volume. After a Gamma-function normalization and a Mellin transform, it reproduces the complete leading WKB grand potential in the examples admitting a suitable Fermi-gas description, including the R-charge and mass deformations compatible with that description. In particular, the transform recovers membrane-instanton series even for $\mathbb{C}^4$, which has no compact curves. For resolved CY$_4$ cones with compact curves, the match requires a quantum refinement of the classical mesonic constraint. We establish the exact equalities of the corresponding dressed disk partition functions with the classical spectral traces for ABJM, ADHM and a representative set of flavored M2-brane quiver theories. Away from the deformation loci where this match can be established, the same construction yields geometric predictions for the membrane-instanton sector that remain to be tested.

</details>


### [133] [Background Effects on Thermal Free Energy in Conformal Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07312)
*Shuichi Yokoyama,Ibuki Yoshida,Hiroki Yunoto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了具有全局阿贝尔对称性的自由共形场论在一般维度下、无限大盒子中热自由能的背景修正，通过引入平坦规范场实现；并将结果应用于U(N)最大超对称杨-米尔斯理论、ABJ(M)理论和二维自由U(N)矢量模型，并指出当背景场增大时热自由能单调上升并穿过零点，对应一个类似色禁闭的相变，且该相变在物质场数超过临界值时必然发生；特别讨论了该二维相变如何规避Mermin-Wagner-Coleman定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有全局对称性的共形场论在有限温度和背景规范场下的热力学行为，尤其是探索是否存在类色禁闭的相变，并检验低维系统中相变与对称性破缺定理的兼容性。

Method: 在无限大盒子中对自由共形场论施加平坦规范场，计算热自由能的背景修正；将该方法应用于多个具体理论（U(N) SYM、ABJ(M)、自由矢量模型）以分析其热自由能随背景场的变化行为及零点特征。

Result: 热自由能在背景场增大时单调增加并穿过零点；该零点对应一个相变，其有效自由度下降，类似于色禁闭；相变发生的条件是物质场数目超过某一临界值；二维情形下该相变可规避Mermin-Wagner-Coleman定理。

Conclusion: 存在一类由背景规范场诱导的热力学相变，其普适特征与色禁闭相似，且在足够多物质场时必然发生；该机制为低维系统中实现非平凡相变提供了新途径。

Abstract: We compute background corrections to the thermal free energy of free conformal field theories (CFTs) with a global abelian symmetry defined on a box with all sides infinitely long by turning on a flat gauge field in general dimensions. This result is applied to the U(N) maximally supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, the ABJ(M) theory, and the free U(N) vector model in four, three, and two spacetime dimensions, respectively. In these examples, the thermal free energies monotonically increase as the background field does, and cross a zero within the valid range. We argue that the systems undergo a phase transition at the zero, where the effective degrees of freedom fall off similarly to the color-confinement one, and that it always occurs if the number of matter fields is greater than a critical value. We comment for this phase transition to circumvent the Mermin-Wagner-Coleman theorem in two spacetime dimensions.

</details>


### [134] [Non-symmetric integral geometry: alternative reconstruction procedure with dual Radon transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07376)
*I. V. Anikin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用非对称积分几何原理，推广了傅里叶切片定理和对偶Radon变换的关系，以支持原函数的替代重建，尤其影响图像重建过程，并揭示了因积分测度不对称导致的反演算子复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展经典傅里叶切片定理与对偶Radon变换关系，以支持更灵活的函数重建，尤其在图像重建中应对传统对称假设的局限。

Method: 基于非对称积分几何（非积分几何）及相应的非对称限制积分测度，推导广义变换关系。

Result: 获得了推广的变换关系，证实了反演算子因积分测度对称性破缺而具有更高复杂性。

Conclusion: 该推广为原函数的替代重建提供了新工具，显著影响图像重建流程，并凸显非对称测度下反问题求解的理论挑战。

Abstract: In the note, we use the principles of non-symmetric integral geometry (non-integral geometry), related to the non-symmetric restricted integration measure, to extend the well-known relations traced from the Fourier slice theorem and the dual Radon transform. The proposed generalization is a very useful for the alternative restoration of the outset function. The latter impacts entirely on the image reconstruction process. The derived relations confirm the complexity of the inversion operator thanks for the symmetry breaking of integration measure.

</details>


### [135] [Improved method for extracting low-energy expansion from OPE in mass-gapped theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07389)
*Hajime Furuta,Yukinari Sumino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Takaura提出的新方法，用于从算符乘积展开（OPE）中提取低能展开的任意阶非零系数，并通过两种新方法在二维大N O(N)非线性σ模型中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Takaura先前的方法仅能提取低能展开的第一非零系数，本文旨在将其推广至任意阶系数，以更全面地研究有质量间隙和单一阈值的渐近自由理论。

Method: 提出了两种新方法：方法I使用特定权重函数直接从OPE中分离出低能展开各阶系数；方法II则先减去‘对偶性破坏’（DV）项再提取系数。

Result: 在二维大N O(N)非线性σ模型中，两种方法均成功重构了高阶低能展开系数。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效扩展了Takaura原始方法的适用范围，为精确提取OPE中多阶低能系数提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Recently, Takaura proposed a new method for extracting the first nonzero coefficient of the low-energy expansion of an observable from its operator product expansion (OPE) in asymptotically free theories with a mass gap and a single threshold. The method uses a weight function to suppress the contribution of the ``duality-violation'' (DV) term that arises when relating the OPE to the low-energy expansion. We extend this method to low-energy expansion coefficients at arbitrary orders. We present two approaches: Method I uses weight functions that isolate the coefficients of low-energy expansion from the OPE. Method II subtracts the DV contribution from the OPE before the extraction. Applying these methods to the two-dimensional large-$N$ $O(N)$ nonlinear sigma model, we find that both methods successfully reconstruct higher-order low-energy expansion coefficients.

</details>


### [136] [$1/N$ corrections to $\langle TJJ\rangle$ and $\langle TTT\rangle$ in the critical $O(N)$ vector and Gross--Neveu--Yukawa models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07394)
*Yongwei Guo,Zhijin Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了三维临界O(N)矢量模型和Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型中⟨TJJ⟩和⟨TTT⟩三点函数在1/N展开下的次领头阶修正，确定了两个宇称偶 conformal 结构的系数及相应 conformal collider 参数，并与数值bootstrap结果进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合量子临界系统中的能量动量张量和电流之间的关联行为，尤其是通过 conformal collider 理论和 bootstrap 约束检验大N方法的可靠性。

Method: 采用动量空间费曼图在软外动量下的展开，结合背景规范场与度规源所需的接触项；同时独立地利用球面自由能对度规及混合度规-规范场的导数进行计算。

Result: 得到了⟨TJJ⟩和⟨TTT⟩三点函数中两个宇称偶 conformal 结构的系数及对应的 conformal collider 参数 γ 和 t₄；在 N=2 时，预测值与bootstrap边界偏差分别为3.28%和4.44%，符合较弱谱假设下的联合bootstrap区域。

Conclusion: 大N展开在定量描述临界O(2)模型的三点函数方面具有较高精度，支持其用于研究量子临界点附近的能量流、高频电导率和剪切响应等物理量。

Abstract: We compute the subleading corrections in the $1/N$ expansion of the three-point functions $\langle TJJ\rangle$ and $\langle TTT\rangle$ in the three-dimensional critical $O(N)$ vector and Gross--Neveu--Yukawa models. We determine the coefficients of the two parity-even conformal structures and the corresponding conformal collider parameters. The calculation uses an expansion of momentum-space Feynman diagrams in a soft external momentum, together with the contact terms required by background gauge and metric sources. The sums of the structure coefficients reproduce the known current and stress-tensor two-point normalizations. We also compute these coefficients independently using the metric and mixed metric--gauge derivatives of the sphere free energy. Extrapolating the large $N$ results to the $O(2)$ vector model yields reasonably good agreement with recent numerical bootstrap bounds. At $N=2$, our $γ$ and $t_4$ predictions lie within the joint bootstrap region under weaker spectral assumptions; their deviations from the more restrictive interval midpoints are only $3.28\%$ and $4.44\%$, respectively. We discuss the implications for energy flux, high-frequency conductivity, and shear response near quantum criticality.

</details>


### [137] [Worldsheet skeins and block decomposition of quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07455)
*Sachin Chauhan,Piotr Kucharski,Lukas Nakamura,Dmitry Noshchenko,Ramadevi Pichai,Vivek Kumar Singh,Marko Stošić*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于结-箭图对应关系，研究HOMFLYPT skein关系的箭图类比，提出并验证了一个关于skein三元组中各链环对应箭图邻接矩阵块分解结构的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索HOMFLYPT skein关系在箭图表示下的结构体现，拓展结-箭图对应到更一般的全纯世界面几何。

Method: 利用全纯世界面及其多重覆盖贡献解释链环不变量，并对skein三元组中各链环对应的箭图邻接矩阵进行块分解分析。

Result: 提出并验证了skein三元组中箭图邻接矩阵存在由其余两个链环对应箭图构成的对角块的结构猜想（模箭图等价）。

Conclusion: 该块分解结构揭示了skein关系在箭图层面的内在代数表现，为理解量子不变量与箭图表示间的深层联系提供了新视角。

Abstract: Following the knot-quiver correspondence, we investigate quiver counterparts of the HOMFLYPT skein relations by interpreting link invariants in terms of holomorphic worldsheets and their multicover contributions. Extending this framework to more general worldsheet geometries, we conjecture that, up to quiver equivalence, the adjacency matrix of the quiver associated with any link in a skein triple admits a block decomposition whose diagonal blocks are quiver adjacency matrices associated with the other two links. We verify this conjecture in numerous examples.

</details>


### [138] [The logarithm construction and (super) Weyl anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07547)
*Sergei M. Kuzenko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用Butter等人提出的高阶导数共形原初（超）场的对数构造方法，推导出适用于多种情形的简单Wess-Zumino作用量，包括任意偶数维Weyl反常、四维N=1和N=2超Weyl反常，以及二维(p,q)超Weyl反常（p,q≤2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一处理不同维度和超对称性下的Weyl反常，需构造简洁且普适的Wess-Zumino作用量。

Method: 采用Butter等人提出的高阶导数共形原初（超）场的对数构造方法。

Result: 成功导出任意偶数维Weyl反常、四维N=1/N=2超Weyl反常及二维(p,q)超Weyl反常（p,q≤2）的简单Wess-Zumino作用量。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种系统、简洁的框架，可用于构造各类Weyl与超Weyl反常对应的Wess-Zumino作用量。

Abstract: We apply the logarithm construction of higher-derivative conformal primary (super) fields proposed by Butter et al. to derive simple Wess-Zumino actions for: (i) Weyl anomalies in arbitrary even dimensions; (ii) $\mathcal{N} =1$ and $\mathcal{N}=2$ super-Weyl anomalies in four dimensions; and (iii) $(p,q)$ super-Weyl anomalies in two dimensions, with $p,q \leq 2$.

</details>


### [139] [ADE Quiver Duality Proposals: Graph Classification and Nodewise Realizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07557)
*Yuanyuan Fang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用大N指数策略发现了四维N=1超对称规范理论中新的quiver对偶性，将对偶性与ADE型Weyl群最长元联系起来，并通过节点逐次对偶序列实现同时对偶，提供了有限N一致性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找四维N=1 quiver规范理论中的新对偶性，并建立其与数学结构（如ADE分类、Weyl群）的深层联系。

Method: 采用大N指数策略分析quiver理论；施加有限介子条件约束图结构；构造显式超势以匹配预测谱；将同时对偶映射到ADE Weyl群最长元的约化表达式；分解为子quiver的同步对偶序列。

Result: 发现一类新的ADE型quiver的同时对偶性；给出一致的经典介子谱的显式超势；建立同时对偶与ADE Weyl群最长元之间的对应；提供有限N下可验证的节点逐次对偶实现方式。

Conclusion: 大N指数方法可有效揭示新的强耦合对偶结构；ADE分类和Weyl群对称性自然出现在quiver对偶中；同时对偶可通过有限步骤的局部操作实现，增强了其物理自洽性。

Abstract: We use the large-$N$ index strategy to find new quiver dualities in four-dimensional $\mathcal N=1$ quiver gauge theories. We study gauge quivers with or without an adjoint field at each gauge node, and bifundamental matter between selected nodes with fixed $R$-charges. Imposing the finite-meson condition restricts the allowed gauge graphs to finite ADE diagrams and leads to a new simultaneous duality for ADE quivers. We present an explicit superpotential that produces a uniform classical meson spectrum consistent with the large-$N$ index approach's prediction. We then relate the simultaneous rank transformation to the longest element of the ADE Weyl group, whose reduced expressions organize nodewise duality sequences realizing the simultaneous ADE duality. Realizing the simultaneous ADE duality through such nodewise sequences offers a finite-$N$ consistency check beyond the large-$N$ index construction. Moreover, we realize the simultaneous ADE duality as a sequence of subquiver simultaneous dualities.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum action for heterotic string field theory with quantum L-infinity structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07635)
*Yasu Gen,Hiroshi Kunitomo,Jojiro Totsuka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个满足量子Batalin-Vilkovisky方程的杂化弦场论量子作用量，基于量子L-无穷结构确定了picture-changing算符的插入方法，并通过迭代求解微分方程与IBL-无穷转移推导量子相互作用，最后验证了一圈两点函数与第一量子化结果一致（忽略幽灵极点问题）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为建立自洽的杂化弦场论量子形式，需解决量子BV方程满足性、picture-changing算符系统插入及消除量子修正中的不一致性问题。

Method: 基于玻色模空间三角剖分，利用量子L-无穷结构确定picture-changing算符插入规则；通过迭代求解微分方程并应用IBL-无穷转移构造高阶量子相互作用；在一阶近似下计算一圈两点函数。

Result: 成功构造满足量子BV方程的量子作用量；给出普适的量子相互作用构造方案；一阶一圈两点函数结果与第一量子化一致（暂未处理spurious poles）。

Conclusion: 该框架为杂化弦场论提供了严格的量子化路径，将经典L-无穷推广至量子IBL-无穷结构，是迈向完整量子弦场论的重要一步。

Abstract: We construct a quantum action for heterotic string field theory that satisfies the quantum Batalin-Vilkovisky equation. Assuming a triangulation of the bosonic moduli space, the prescription for inserting picture-changing operators is determined based on a quantum L-infinity structure. After concretely constructing some of the lower-order interactions, we propose a general prescription, which generalizes that for constructing the classical interactions governed by a classical L-infinity structure. Assuming it does not suffer from spurious poles, all quantum interactions are determined by iteratively solving differential equations and transforming the solutions via IBL-infinity-transfer. To demonstrate the consistency of our prescription, we compute the one-loop two-point functions (modulo the spurious pole issue) and show that they agree with those in the first-quantized formulation.

</details>


### [141] [Topological Characterization of Adinkra Heights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07637)
*Amanda E. Francis,S. James Gates,Digvijay Roy Varier,Ursula A. Whitcher*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对具有1至4种边颜色的超立方体Adinkra高度进行了分类，利用图论等价概念，并建立了与修正的扭曲特征类之间的双射关系，提供了比HYMN更精细的分类，同时将该分类与二维手性和扭曲手性超多重态的物理联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adinkras是封装一维超对称表示的高度结构化二分图，但其高度函数的分类尚不完善，特别是多边颜色情况下的等价类划分和物理意义需要进一步研究。

Method: 采用图论中的等价概念对超立方体Adinkra高度进行分类，并通过构造修正的扭曲特征类建立双射关系，同时分析其与二维超对称物理模型的关联。

Result: 完成了对1至4种边颜色超立方体Adinkra高度的完整分类；在四色情形下识别出990个不同高度函数，并证明其等价类与修正扭曲特征类一一对应；该分类比HYMN更精细；揭示了其与二维手性/扭曲手性超多重态的物理联系。

Conclusion: Adinkra高度的图论分类不仅具有数学结构性意义，还为理解低维超对称表示及其物理实现提供了新工具和更精细的刻画方式。

Abstract: Adinkras are highly structured bipartite graphs that encapsulate 1D supersymmetry representations. We classify hypercube Adinkra heights with one, two, three or four edge colors using a graph-theoretic notion of equivalence. For the 990 distinct height functions in the case of four edge colors, we show that these classes match modified twisted character classes bijectively and provide a finer classification than spectral Height Yielding Matrix Numbers (HYMNs). We also relate our classification to the physics of the two-dimensional chiral and twisted chiral supermultiplets.

</details>


### [142] [The Mass Gap Approach to QCD. III. Solution of the gluon confinement problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07897)
*Vakhtang Gogokhia,Gergely Gábor Barnaföldi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于质量间隙方法解决QCD中的胶子禁闭问题和渐近自由区域的尺度破缺问题，推导出精确的全胶子传播子表达式，能求和所有严重红外奇异性，并通过复变函数理论与正确实现的维数正规化方法处理这些奇异性；尽管存在质量间隙，胶子仍保持无质量，且解满足长程主导、不进入物理谱及短程渐近自由等边界条件，并讨论了与Elitzur定理的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决QCD中的胶子禁闭问题和解释渐近自由 regime 中的尺度破缺现象。

Method: 基于先前提出的质量间隙方法，推导全胶子传播子的精确表达式；结合复变函数理论与正确嵌入分布理论的维数正规化方法处理严重红外奇异性。

Result: 获得满足长程主导、防止胶子进入物理谱、短程渐近自由等边界条件的胶子传播子解；证明尽管存在质量间隙，胶子仍为无质量；并与Elitzur定理建立联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解QCD真空结构、胶子禁闭机制及渐近自由提供了新的严格理论框架，强调质量间隙在非微扰QCD中的核心作用。

Abstract: We present reports on progress in the solution of the gluon confinement problem and in the explanation of the scale breaking in the asymptotic freedom regime in QCD. It is based on the previously formulated the mass gap approach to QCD, which sheds the light on a mass origin in its ground state. We have established the exact expression for the full gluon propagator. One of its solution allows to sum up all the severe (more singular than $1/q^2$) infrared singularities which can be accommodated by a single full gluon propagator. In order to correctly treat them, we have developed a formalism of the joint use of the theory of functions of complex variable, and the dimensional regularization method correctly implemented into the theory of distributions. Despite the presence of the mass gap, gluons remain massless. Our solution satisfies the two boundary conditions: it dominates over all other possible solutions at large distances but itself prevents gluons to appear in the physical spectrum. It possesses the asymptotic freedom behaviour at short distances. We discuss the connection of our results to the Elitzur's theorem.

</details>


### [143] [Accessing Fractional Wilson Lines from U(1) Monodromy Defects on the Fuzzy Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08102)
*Yin Tang,W. Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维O(2) Wilson-Fisher共形场论中一族U(1)单值缺陷，该缺陷对应于临界非紧致阿贝尔希格斯模型中的分数Wilson线；通过模糊球框架引入分数Aharonov-Bohm通量，发现具有分数横向自旋的带电算符及受保护维度的位移算符，证实其共形性；未发现保持对称性的相关缺陷标量，而电荷为一的磁单极子算符是破坏对称性的相关扰动；结果为分数Wilson线在临界阿贝尔希格斯模型中是稳定的共形线提供了非微扰证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wilson线作为规范不变探针可用于探测电荷屏蔽和退禁闭现象，但在强耦合临界点处其普适信息难以获取。

Method: 研究三维O(2) Wilson-Fisher CFT中连续U(1)单值缺陷族，利用模糊球框架实现该缺陷（通过在球两极引入分数Aharonov-Bohm通量），分析其谱结构与算符性质。

Result: 发现具有分数横向自旋的带电算符；位移算符处于受保护维度，支持缺陷线的共形性；未发现相关对称性保持缺陷标量；电荷为一的磁单极子算符是相关对称性破缺扰动。

Conclusion: 基于O(2) CFT与临界非紧致阿贝尔希格斯模型的对偶性，该工作为分数Wilson线在后者中构成稳定共形线提供了非微扰证据。

Abstract: Wilson lines are gauge-invariant probes of charge screening and deconfinement, which can detect the response of quantum critical matter to an external charge, but their universal information at strongly-coupled critical points remains hard to access. Here we investigate a continuous family of $U(1)$ monodromy defects of the three-dimensional $O(2)$ Wilson--Fisher conformal field theory, which is dual to fractional Wilson lines in the critical noncompact Abelian Higgs model with one unit-charged scalar. We realize this continuous family of defects within the fuzzy-sphere framework by threading a fractional Aharonov--Bohm flux through the two poles of the sphere. We observe the emergence of charged operators carrying fractional transverse spins, as a characteristic signature of the imposed monodromy. The appearance of displacement operator at its protected dimension supports the conformal nature of this defect line. Crucially, we resolve no relevant symmetry-preserving defect scalar, and identify charge-one monopole operators as relevant symmetry-breaking deformations. Assuming the duality, these results provide non-perturbative evidence that fractional Wilson lines of the critical Abelian Higgs model are stable conformal lines.

</details>


### [144] [Pohlmeyer reduction of Newton-Cartan strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08164)
*Aritra Banerjee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了非相对论弦在弦牛顿-卡坦背景下的Pohlmeyer型约化方法，针对球面上的弦，识别出不变约化场，证明其动力学可积并构造了Lax表示；同时建立了非相对论弦解与其约化形式之间的字典关系，明确了哪些相对论孤子解在非相对论极限下存留或消失，并为牛顿-卡坦弦动力学应用可积性方法提供了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于非相对论弦理论的可积性分析工具，理解非相对论极限下相对论孤子结构的存留与消亡机制，并建立非相对论弦动力学与可积系统之间的联系。

Method: 采用Pohlmeyer型约化方法处理经典非相对论弦在弦牛顿-卡坦背景上的运动，特别聚焦于二维球面情形；通过构造不变约化场、推导其运动方程、建立Lax对来证明可积性，并与相对论弦的非相对论展开进行比对以建立解的对应字典。

Result: 成功实现非相对论弦的Pohlmeyer约化；确认约化场的动力学可积并给出Lax表示；建立非相对论弦解与约化场解之间的明确对应关系；明确识别出在非相对论极限下存留或解耦的相对论孤子构型。

Conclusion: 该约化框架不仅揭示了非相对论弦系统的内在可积结构，还为系统应用可积性技术研究牛顿-卡坦几何中的弦动力学奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop a Pohlmeyer-type reduction for classical non-relativistic strings on string Newton-Cartan backgrounds. For strings on a two-sphere, we identify the invariant reduced field, show that its dynamics is integrable, and construct a Lax representation. We relate this intrinsic formulation to the non-relativistic expansion of relativistic strings and obtain a dictionary between non-relativistic string solutions and their reduced counterparts. The construction explicitly determines which relativistic solitonic configurations survive the non-relativistic limit and which decouple. Moreover, it provides a framework for applying integrability methods to Newton-Cartan string dynamics.

</details>


### [145] [The three-dimensional Galilean black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08471)
*Luis Avilés,Oscar Fuentealba,Diego Hidalgo,Pablo Rodríguez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三维伽利略反德西特引力的新解，该理论可表述为在三维双扩展牛顿-胡克群下不变的拓扑规范理论；解具有能量、角动量、自旋和静质量四个守恒荷，且自旋大小受静质量上限约束；该解具备热性质，可视为规范理论框架下的黑洞构型，并通过欧氏延拓的拓扑正则性条件导出温度、角速度等化学势；零温极限下，当自旋-静质量界限饱和时出现极值构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建三维伽利略反德西特引力的新型精确解，并探索其作为黑洞类构型的热力学与对称性本质。

Method: 将理论表述为基于双扩展牛顿-胡克群的三维拓扑规范理论，通过求解场方程获得具有四个守恒荷的精确解，并利用欧氏延拓与正则性条件分析其热力学量。

Result: 获得一个具有能量、角动量、自旋和静质量四守恒荷的三维新解；发现自旋受静质量上限约束；推导出温度、角速度等热力学量；证明零温极限下存在自旋-静质量界限饱和的极值黑洞构型。

Conclusion: 该解不仅拓展了非相对论引力理论中的黑洞概念，还揭示了伽利略对称性下自旋与质量之间的基本约束关系，为理解非相对论极限下的量子引力提供了新视角。

Abstract: We report a novel solution of three-dimensional Galilean Anti-de Sitter gravity. The theory can be formulated as a topological gauge theory invariant under the doubly extended Newton-Hooke group in three dimensions. The solution possesses four conserved charges: energy, angular momentum, spin and rest mass. Interestingly, the magnitude of the spin turns out to bounded from above by the rest mass. The solution exhibits the thermal properties that define a black hole configuration in terms of its gauge theoretical description. Temperature, angular velocity and other chemical potentials are obtained from suitable regularity conditions associated with the topology of the Euclidean continuation of the solution. At zero temperature, an extreme configuration is shown to emerge provided the bound between the spin and the rest mass saturates.

</details>


### [146] [Defect Anomalies at a Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08476)
*Oleksandr Diatlyk,Zimo Sun,Yifan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究表面缺陷携带的Weyl和引力反常，揭示其如何通过体应力-张量关联函数远距离编码，并推导出基于缺陷算符数据的反常求和规则及三维CFT中边界Weyl反常的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面缺陷上的Weyl与引力反常推广了二维共形场论的中心荷，刻画了无能隙表面模式对背景几何的反常响应，但其远距离编码机制尚不明确。

Method: 通过分析scheme-independent的体应力-张量关联函数，推导反常的积分表示与求和规则，并结合缺陷算符数据建立理论约束。

Result: 发现表面缺陷反常可由体应力-张量关联函数远距离编码；推导出基于缺陷算符的反常求和规则；证明三维CFT中边界Weyl反常存在严格下界；并通过具体例子验证结论。

Conclusion: 表面缺陷的几何反常不仅局域体现为接触项，更全局地编码于体关联函数中，这为理解边界/缺陷反常提供了新视角，并建立了普适性约束。

Abstract: Surface defects carry Weyl and gravitational anomalies that generalize the central charges of two-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs) and characterize the anomalous response of gapless surface modes to background geometry. These anomalies usually appear as contact terms on the defect. We explain how they are encoded at a distance, in scheme-independent integrated bulk stress-tensor correlators. We derive sum rules for them in terms of defect operator data, establish a lower bound on the boundary Weyl anomaly in three-dimensional CFTs, and illustrate our results with explicit examples.

</details>
